# phase-09 完整对话

分配 413 词形；已接受 413，暂缓 0，未决 0。

“已接受”仅指本版本存在完整、绑定文本哈希的独立署名审阅记录，不是程序证明语义正确。

<a id="dialogue-p09-a-01"></a>

## 约定隔离会堂入口台阶并检查扶手底座 · p09-a-01

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：会堂入口台阶隔离与扶手底座检查安排（heritage-step-handrail-repair-scope）。

沟通目标：讨论加固扶手底座与重建整个入口的可能性，约定先隔离入口台阶再请维修人员检查扶手底座

背景：社区旧会堂的管理员与维修协调员检查入口台阶。

人物：Jamie、Lin。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-01-s01 · Jamie: The rail moves when I put my hand on it. We should close these steps.
- p09-a-01-s02 · Lin: Yes. We may be able to reinforce the base, but someone needs to inspect it first.
- p09-a-01-s03 · Jamie: Do you think we will have to rebuild the whole entrance?
- p09-a-01-s04 · Lin: I can't tell from here. The other entrance is open, so there is no need to guess.
- p09-a-01-s05 · Jamie: I'll secure the temporary barrier across these steps and put up a sign.
- p09-a-01-s06 · Lin: Good. Once that's done, I'll ask our repair team to inspect the base and explain the options.
- p09-a-01-s07 · Jamie: Agreed. We won't reopen this entrance until they say it's ready.

目标用法：

- rail · n. · 栏杆；扶手；义项 sense-staged-ox-2468bc42e8ded047-p09-a-01；来源 ox-2468bc42e8ded047；证据 p09-a-01-s01；词形 rail。
- rebuild · v. · 重建；义项 sense-staged-ox-bb93d642f6a46074-p09-a-01；来源 ox-bb93d642f6a46074；证据 p09-a-01-s03；词形 rebuild。
- reinforce · v. · 加固；义项 sense-staged-ox-a3d5d31c0027c6e8-p09-a-01；来源 ox-a3d5d31c0027c6e8；证据 p09-a-01-s02；词形 reinforce。
- secure · v. · 把某物固定牢靠；义项 sense-staged-ox-565f87a3eff3612b-p09-a-01；来源 ox-565f87a3eff3612b；证据 p09-a-01-s05；词形 secure。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

完整重读版本2：s01观察扶手晃动，s02只讨论加固底座的可能，s03询问整个入口重建，s04拒绝未经检查作判断。新标题正确把隔离对象写为入口台阶、检查对象写为扶手底座；新目标分开底座加固与整个入口重建，并以约定对应s05–s07的封闭、联系维修队与暂不重开承诺。任务连贯，未声称检查或维修已经完成。

绑定文件 SHA-256：f986d21806f70d8a094218066bf3ae0f3c7509fbd715074643ed8743dfaa8424

- ox-2468bc42e8ded047：accepted；重读s01，The rail作名词主语，手放上即晃动以及入口台阶背景明确为扶手；不是铁轨义。（p09-a-01-s01）。
- ox-bb93d642f6a46074：accepted；重读s03，have to rebuild the whole entrance用动词指重建整个入口，疑问与s04未知回应保留未决定状态；版本2目标不再误指重建扶手。（p09-a-01-s03）。
- ox-a3d5d31c0027c6e8：accepted；重读s02，reinforce the base为加固底座的及物动词搭配，may be able与先检查的条件使建议范围准确。（p09-a-01-s02）。
- ox-565f87a3eff3612b：accepted；重读s05，secure the temporary barrier across these steps表示把临时围栏固定牢靠，动词宾语及位置明确，行动仍是承诺。（p09-a-01-s05）。

<a id="dialogue-p09-a-02"></a>

## 商定课程调查的评分题与邀请方式 · p09-a-02

主题：研究与论证；情景：课程满意度问题设计（course-survey-rating-question-design）。

沟通目标：区分课程评分与活动排序，决定采用单项评分题并随机邀请学员

背景：两位课程组织者拟定课后问卷，尚未发送。

人物：Nadia、Evan。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-02-s01 · Nadia: How should we measure satisfaction with the course?
- p09-a-02-s02 · Evan: Would you like people to rank the activities from best to worst?
- p09-a-02-s03 · Nadia: That tells us which activity they prefer, not whether they liked the course overall.
- p09-a-02-s04 · Evan: Then ask for one rating from one to five, followed by an optional comment.
- p09-a-02-s05 · Nadia: Good. We should invite a random sample of students, not just the people who stayed to chat.
- p09-a-02-s06 · Evan: Agreed. I'll use the full attendance list to draw the names, and we'll keep the question about the course as a whole.

目标用法：

- random · adj. · 随机选择的；义项 sense-staged-ox-442399e4ef164333-p09-a-02；来源 ox-442399e4ef164333；证据 p09-a-02-s05；词形 random。
- rank · v. · 把事物按顺序排列；义项 sense-staged-ox-1255fc076e3c5104-p09-a-02；来源 ox-1255fc076e3c5104；证据 p09-a-02-s02；词形 rank。
- rating · n. · 评分；义项 sense-staged-ox-16e612c371ddf774-p09-a-02；来源 ox-16e612c371ddf774；证据 p09-a-02-s04；词形 rating。
- satisfaction · n. · 满意程度；义项 sense-staged-ox-5c1fa0a1c6f699a9-p09-a-02；来源 ox-5c1fa0a1c6f699a9；证据 p09-a-02-s01；词形 satisfaction。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

从整体课程满意度转向活动排序的区别明确，最终决定一至五分单项评分及随机邀请方式。标题和目标描述的是商定问卷方案，s06保留未来抽取名单，未声称已发送或收回问卷。

绑定文件 SHA-256：ccf35857fb9d5a8e7037070b7a6ae959bf23cef8b79668ea88a9bb3e92c42232

- ox-442399e4ef164333：accepted；s05的random sample修饰学员样本，s06从完整出席名单抽名字支持随机选择义，而非随意挑熟人。（p09-a-02-s05）。
- ox-1255fc076e3c5104：accepted；s02中rank the activities from best to worst是及物排序动词，s03明确区分活动排序与课程整体满意度。（p09-a-02-s02）。
- ox-16e612c371ddf774：accepted；s04中one rating from one to five的可数名词指一个量表评分，不是排序名次。（p09-a-02-s04）。
- ox-5c1fa0a1c6f699a9：accepted；s01中measure satisfaction with the course以不可数名词表示对课程的满意程度，搭配自然。（p09-a-02-s01）。

## 发现厨房老鼠后约定停用与报修 · p09-a-03

主题：居家生活；情景：共用厨房害虫报告（shared-kitchen-pest-report）。

沟通目标：说明老鼠出现的位置，约定停用厨房并联系管理员处理暴露食材和害虫

背景：合租者在共用厨房发现害虫，商量通知管理员。

人物：Hana、Luis。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-03-s01 · Hana: I just saw a rat go behind the fridge.
- p09-a-03-s02 · Luis: Was the bowl of raw vegetables on the counter uncovered?
- p09-a-03-s03 · Hana: Yes. I won't use those for dinner. We need to tell everyone not to use the kitchen for now.
- p09-a-03-s04 · Luis: I'll call the building manager and ask how they plan to get rid of the rats.
- p09-a-03-s05 · Hana: Please mention the uncovered food too. I do not want us guessing which things are safe to keep.
- p09-a-03-s06 · Luis: I will. You message the house group, and I'll ask the manager to arrange a visit.

目标用法：

- raw · adj. · 未烹煮的；生的；义项 sense-staged-ox-11c5cb4021e3c441-p09-a-03；来源 ox-11c5cb4021e3c441；证据 p09-a-03-s02；词形 raw。
- rat · n. · 老鼠；义项 sense-staged-ox-c950e5dd755fa7bd-p09-a-03；来源 ox-c950e5dd755fa7bd；证据 p09-a-03-s01；词形 rat。
- rid · v. · 使摆脱；除去；义项 sense-staged-ox-b19c584094871555-p09-a-03；来源 ox-b19c584094871555；证据 p09-a-03-s04；词形 rid。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

合租者从具体目击位置谈到裸露食物及停用厨房，结尾分工通知住户和联系管理员。标题与目标均是约定后续措施，不把拟联系管理员误写成已经除虫；不擅自给食品安全判断。

绑定文件 SHA-256：3672d1519b1190564adffb762d24266f422d8b695705ecd2fc9a8d7eae8c5a07

- ox-11c5cb4021e3c441：accepted；s02中raw vegetables形容未烹煮蔬菜，后续食材暴露问题与该义不冲突。（p09-a-03-s02）。
- ox-c950e5dd755fa7bd：accepted；s01中a rat是看到钻到冰箱后面的老鼠，单数可数名词自然；后文泛指鼠患合理。（p09-a-03-s01）。
- ox-b19c584094871555：accepted；s04中get rid of the rats使用固定表达表示除去鼠患；所选rid动词来源及中文除去义适合此结构。（p09-a-03-s04）。

## 订单增长后商定分批交付提议 · p09-a-04

主题：工作与协作；情景：快速增长订单的交期核对（rapid-order-growth-deadline-check）。

沟通目标：依据包装能力确定可合理承诺的数量，并决定向客户提出分批交付

背景：两位小型印刷工作室同事在回复客户前核对产能。

人物：Priya、Dan。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-04-s01 · Priya: The client wants a rapid response about the delivery date.
- p09-a-04-s02 · Dan: Orders are coming in rapidly, but we still have only two people packing them.
- p09-a-04-s03 · Priya: Is Friday a realistic date for all two hundred sets?
- p09-a-04-s04 · Dan: No. We can reasonably promise a hundred by Friday. The rest would need until Monday.
- p09-a-04-s05 · Priya: I reckon they could use the first hundred at the opening and the rest next week.
- p09-a-04-s06 · Dan: Maybe, but let's ask rather than assume. Offer those two dates and explain the quantities.
- p09-a-04-s07 · Priya: Agreed. I'll send that proposal now and wait for their answer before confirming the job.

目标用法：

- realistic · adj. · 现实可行的；义项 sense-staged-ox-edc6a58f112a5bb3-p09-a-04；来源 ox-edc6a58f112a5bb3；证据 p09-a-04-s03；词形 realistic。
- reasonably · adv. · 合理地；有充分依据地；义项 sense-staged-ox-f9eccb79baca4684-p09-a-04；来源 ox-f9eccb79baca4684；证据 p09-a-04-s04；词形 reasonably。
- reckon · v. · 认为；估计；义项 sense-staged-ox-810119a736726f14-p09-a-04；来源 ox-810119a736726f14；证据 p09-a-04-s05；词形 reckon。
- rapid · adj. · 迅速的；义项 sense-staged-ox-529ea23be946a119-p09-a-04；来源 ox-529ea23be946a119；证据 p09-a-04-s01；词形 rapid。
- rapidly · adv. · 迅速地；义项 sense-staged-ox-192e4f387f07e4b7-p09-a-04；来源 ox-192e4f387f07e4b7；证据 p09-a-04-s02；词形 rapidly。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

产能约束解释了两百套不能全在周五交付，双方明确提出一百套周五、余量周一并等待客户答复。标题、目标都是拟定分批交付提议，不宣称客户已接受。

- ox-f9eccb79baca4684：accepted；s04的reasonably修饰promise，意为依据能力合理承诺，而不是程度上的相当。（p09-a-04-s04）。
- ox-810119a736726f14：accepted；s05中I reckon后接从句表示说话者估计客户可以分批使用，s06提醒需询问而非当成事实。（p09-a-04-s05）。
- ox-529ea23be946a119：accepted；s01中rapid response以形容词修饰回复，表示客户要求快速回应交期。（p09-a-04-s01）。

## 换位置试听后决定保留旧收音机 · p09-a-05

主题：消费与服务；情景：旧收音机信号试听（radio-receiver-reception-check）。

沟通目标：比较不同位置的接收效果，决定保留设备并仅在杂音再次出现时联系店员

背景：顾客带旧收音机到维修店，店员与她一起试听。

人物：Marta、Joel。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-05-s01 · Marta: I bought this radio receiver here last year. Do you recall showing me how to tune it?
- p09-a-05-s02 · Joel: I do. What has changed?
- p09-a-05-s03 · Marta: The signal is relatively weak in my kitchen compared with the bedroom.
- p09-a-05-s04 · Joel: Let's try it away from the metal shelf. There, can you hear the station now?
- p09-a-05-s05 · Marta: Yes, that's remarkably clear. The kitchen radio sits right beside a large metal cabinet.
- p09-a-05-s06 · Joel: Try another position at home before paying for a repair. If the noise returns, bring it back.
- p09-a-05-s07 · Marta: I will. I'll keep this one and contact you if moving it doesn't solve the problem at home.

目标用法：

- recall · v. · 记得；回想起；义项 sense-staged-ox-be19e60c37fe05c9-p09-a-05；来源 ox-be19e60c37fe05c9；证据 p09-a-05-s01；词形 recall。
- receiver · n. · 无线电接收机；义项 sense-staged-ox-f6d28943437b3ca4-p09-a-05；来源 ox-f6d28943437b3ca4；证据 p09-a-05-s01；词形 receiver。
- relatively · adv. · 相对而言；义项 sense-staged-ox-022e7d3feaee52c9-p09-a-05；来源 ox-022e7d3feaee52c9；证据 p09-a-05-s03；词形 relatively。
- remarkably · adv. · 非常；出乎意料地；义项 sense-staged-ox-92855aed1f418ee4-p09-a-05；来源 ox-92855aed1f418ee4；证据 p09-a-05-s05；词形 remarkably。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

顾客比较家中两个房间，店内移离金属架后现场试听清晰，结尾决定保留收音机并回家再试。标题和目标未把店内改善说成家中问题已解决；仅在问题持续或重现时联系店员的条件清楚。

- ox-be19e60c37fe05c9：accepted；s01中recall showing me以动名词补语表示记得先前演示调台，s02回答I do承接自然。（p09-a-05-s01）。
- ox-f6d28943437b3ca4：accepted；s01的radio receiver是可数设备名词，后续调台、信号和试听共同限定为无线电接收机。（p09-a-05-s01）。

<a id="dialogue-p09-a-06"></a>

## 为虚构商店案例选择短期营业方案 · p09-a-06

主题：学习与技能；情景：虚构零售案例的收入变化（fictional-shop-revenue-reduction-plan）。

沟通目标：区分营业收入与利润，依据课堂数据选择短期缩短晚间营业的方案

背景：商科课堂中的两名学生分析虚构零售商店，不是在提供经营或投资建议。

人物：Aisha、Ben。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-06-s01 · Aisha: In this scenario, a recession has reduced spending in the town. What does that mean for our shop?
- p09-a-06-s02 · Ben: Retail is the only sector shown here. The case does not give us figures for restaurants or factories.
- p09-a-06-s03 · Aisha: The table shows a twenty percent reduction in revenue. Is that the same as a twenty percent fall in profit?
- p09-a-06-s04 · Ben: No. Revenue is money from sales before costs. We need the cost page to work out profit.
- p09-a-06-s05 · Aisha: Here it is. The late opening hours cost more to run than they bring in.
- p09-a-06-s06 · Ben: Then, for our short-term plan, shall we close at six instead of eight and review the figures after a month?
- p09-a-06-s07 · Aisha: Yes. We can explain that choice using the late-evening figures, rather than claiming every shop should do it.

目标用法：

- recession · n. · 经济衰退；义项 sense-staged-ox-0050329ed1e872c6-p09-a-06；来源 ox-0050329ed1e872c6；证据 p09-a-06-s01；词形 recession。
- retail · n. · 零售业；义项 sense-staged-ox-fe83ee033fb89732-p09-a-06；来源 ox-fe83ee033fb89732；证据 p09-a-06-s02；词形 retail。
- revenue · n. · 营业收入；义项 sense-staged-ox-43e152fe901278d6-p09-a-06；来源 ox-43e152fe901278d6；证据 p09-a-06-s03；词形 revenue。
- reduction · n. · 减少；下降；义项 sense-staged-ox-e3989938e7da5a04-p09-a-06；来源 ox-e3989938e7da5a04；证据 p09-a-06-s03；词形 reduction。
- short-term · adj. · 短期的；义项 sense-staged-ox-eb20c8cb31063562-p09-a-06；来源 ox-eb20c8cb31063562；证据 p09-a-06-s06；词形 short-term。
- scenario · n. · 设定的情境；义项 sense-staged-ox-cb4716b94a640dc8-p09-a-06；来源 ox-cb4716b94a640dc8；证据 p09-a-06-s01；词形 scenario。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

虚构商科案例清楚区分收入和利润，晚间成本资料支撑暂时提前关门并一个月后复核的课堂方案。标题与目标均限定案例中的方案选择，不假称实际经营调整或普遍建议已经实施。

绑定文件 SHA-256：6492d121b4e5c64e59623afd6985198f4342d3d915eebf7f563f65f1dbadf7f0

- ox-0050329ed1e872c6：accepted；s01中a recession作主语，描述案例中的经济衰退降低消费，所选名词义明确。（p09-a-06-s01）。

## 从备份找回录音并试听核对 · p09-a-07

主题：工作与协作；情景：误删录音的备份恢复（deleted-recording-backup-recovery）。

沟通目标：从昨日备份恢复误删录音，并通过播放开头与结尾核对文件内容

背景：工作室同事使用本地项目备份找回误删的访谈录音。

人物：Mina、Omar。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-07-s01 · Mina: I deleted the interview recording by mistake. Can we recover it?
- p09-a-07-s02 · Omar: There is a backup from yesterday. I can restore the file to a separate folder so we do not overwrite anything.
- p09-a-07-s03 · Mina: Please do. The file should start with Rosa introducing herself.
- p09-a-07-s04 · Omar: The recovery has finished. Here is the file; let us play the beginning.
- p09-a-07-s05 · Mina: That's Rosa's introduction. Now check the end, where she thanks the volunteers.
- p09-a-07-s06 · Omar: I can hear that too. The recording runs for the full eighteen minutes.
- p09-a-07-s07 · Mina: Good, we've got the right recording back. I'll copy this checked version into the project folder.

- recovery · n. · 数据恢复；义项 sense-staged-ox-e9b72c5c589830e9-p09-a-07；来源 ox-e9b72c5c589830e9；证据 p09-a-07-s04；词形 recovery。

从删除求助、选择昨日备份到恢复完成及首尾试听形成完整操作链。s04明确恢复完成，s05–s06确认自我介绍、致谢和十八分钟长度，支持标题与目标的找回和核对；复制回项目目录仍只是结尾承诺。

## 核对助理招聘要求并约定准备简历 · p09-a-08

主题：工作与协作；情景：助理职位申请要求澄清（assistant-vacancy-application-clarification）。

沟通目标：确认相关经验可以来自志愿工作，找到招聘咨询人并决定准备针对岗位的简历

- p09-a-08-s01 · Leah: Are you still hoping to recruit an assistant for the weekend workshops?
- p09-a-08-s02 · Marcus: Yes. The recruitment notice is on our website, and applications stay open until Friday.
- p09-a-08-s03 · Leah: My paid work is in a shop. Is my volunteer teaching experience relevant?
- p09-a-08-s04 · Marcus: Yes. We need someone who can explain a task clearly to beginners.
- p09-a-08-s05 · Leah: Should I include that on my résumé rather than listing every shop duty?
- p09-a-08-s06 · Marcus: Include both, but give a clear example from the teaching. Our senior tutor, Eva Chen, can answer questions about the classes.
- p09-a-08-s07 · Leah: Could you point me to her work email on the notice?
- p09-a-08-s08 · Marcus: It is under the heading "Questions about the role."
- p09-a-08-s09 · Leah: Found it. I'll prepare a résumé focused on those examples and contact Eva if I need to clarify the class duties.

## 商定由队长赛后向裁判询问 · p09-a-09

主题：文娱与运动；情景：足球赛后异议沟通边界（football-referee-protest-boundary）。

沟通目标：听取对判罚的不满，约定不与对手争执而由队长赛后私下询问裁判

背景：业余足球比赛暂停时，队长劝队员避免现场争吵。

人物：Tom、Rafi。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-09-s01 · Tom: The referee gave them the ball again. Their player touched it last.
- p09-a-09-s02 · Rafi: I saw it differently, but arguing now will not help us play.
- p09-a-09-s03 · Tom: The rival captain is smiling at me. He knows what happened.
- p09-a-09-s04 · Rafi: You don't know what he is thinking. Try to resist the temptation to shout at him.
- p09-a-09-s05 · Tom: Will you ask about the decision after the match?
- p09-a-09-s06 · Rafi: Yes. As captain, I'll speak to the referee privately. For now, let's get back into position.
- p09-a-09-s07 · Tom: All right. I'll leave it with you and focus on the game.

目标用法：

- referee · n. · 裁判；义项 sense-staged-ox-c7f034a9bc8570a7-p09-a-09；来源 ox-c7f034a9bc8570a7；证据 p09-a-09-s01；词形 referee。
- rival · adj. · 竞争对手的；义项 sense-staged-ox-287be356b8cf220f-p09-a-09；来源 ox-287be356b8cf220f；证据 p09-a-09-s03；词形 rival。
- resist · v. · 克制；抵制；义项 sense-staged-ox-5d0edca512950190-p09-a-09；来源 ox-5d0edca512950190；证据 p09-a-09-s04；词形 resist。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

暂停时队员表达判罚不满，队长制止猜测对方心理，承诺赛后私下询问并让队员回到比赛。标题与目标的赛后询问仅为约定，未误称裁判已经解释或改判；场景标签指拟采用的沟通方式。

## 核对参加条件并完成语言咖啡馆登记 · p09-a-10

- p09-a-10-s05 · Safa: Can I register for next Tuesday, under Safa?
- p09-a-10-s06 · Helen: Yes. Registration is free, and there is still a place.
- p09-a-10-s07 · Safa: Tuesday, please. I'll come to the welcome desk at six.
- p09-a-10-s08 · Helen: I've added Safa to Tuesday's list. Six at the welcome desk is correct.

## 承认公开玩笑的伤害并答应删评 · p09-a-11

主题：家庭与人际；情景：公开玩笑后的道歉与请求（public-joke-apology-request）。

沟通目标：听取朋友对公开评论的感受，承认评论不妥并同意删掉它和不再拿这件事开玩笑

背景：朋友私下讨论一条让当事人难堪的社交媒体评论。

人物：Iris、Sam。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-11-s01 · Iris: Your remark under my photo made me feel ridiculous in front of everyone.
- p09-a-11-s02 · Sam: I meant it as a joke, but I can hear that it hurt you.
- p09-a-11-s03 · Iris: You may regard it as harmless. I am sensitive about losing that job, and you know it.
- p09-a-11-s04 · Sam: I regret posting it. I was not my usual self yesterday, but that is not an excuse.
- p09-a-11-s05 · Iris: Please delete it. I do not want a big public apology that draws more attention to it.
- p09-a-11-s06 · Sam: I will delete the comment and stop joking about the job. Is there anything else you need from me now?
- p09-a-11-s07 · Iris: Just some space. We can talk again later.
- p09-a-11-s08 · Sam: Okay. I won't push you to make me feel better about it.

目标用法：

- regret · v. · 后悔做了某事；义项 sense-staged-ox-ba1ded0f7c3b5fd1-p09-a-11；来源 ox-ba1ded0f7c3b5fd1；证据 p09-a-11-s04；词形 regret。
- regard · v. · 把某事看作；义项 sense-staged-ox-30a8a0b184b05ca7-p09-a-11；来源 ox-30a8a0b184b05ca7；证据 p09-a-11-s03；词形 regard。

- ridiculous · adj. · 可笑的；令人难堪的；义项 sense-staged-ox-de6d494cec1458ea-p09-a-11；来源 ox-de6d494cec1458ea；证据 p09-a-11-s01；词形 ridiculous。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

## 确认气流演示的操作与入场限制 · p09-a-12

主题：学习与技能；情景：工作坊气流设备与进入规则（workshop-airflow-access-rules）。

沟通目标：澄清设备旋钮的用途，依据课堂给定规则决定由讲师操作并把观众留在标线外

背景：虚构科学工作坊筹备中，两人对照教师提供的设备及场地说明，不提供实际设备操作指导。

人物：Theo、Mei。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-12-s01 · Theo: Does this dial regulate the flow of air through the demonstration tube?
- p09-a-12-s02 · Mei: That is what the equipment sheet says. The tutor will operate it during the demonstration.
- p09-a-12-s03 · Theo: Can visitors stand beside the table?
- p09-a-12-s04 · Mei: No. The safety regulation on this room sheet requires a clear space around the equipment.
- p09-a-12-s05 · Theo: So we need to restrict access to the marked area, not close the whole room.
- p09-a-12-s06 · Mei: Exactly. The restriction applies to the area inside the line. Visitors can watch from the seats outside it.
- p09-a-12-s07 · Theo: Then I'll put the seats beyond the line and leave the controls to the tutor.
- p09-a-12-s08 · Mei: Agreed. We will explain that arrangement when the group arrives.

- regulate · v. · 调节；控制流量；义项 sense-staged-ox-9ccebf09b81f05bb-p09-a-12；来源 ox-9ccebf09b81f05bb；证据 p09-a-12-s01；词形 regulate。

<a id="dialogue-p09-a-13"></a>

## 约定接替照护一小时并确认联络方式 · p09-a-13

主题：家庭与人际；情景：照护者短时休息交接（caregiver-break-handover-agreement）。

沟通目标：确认亲人愿意接替一小时，约定回来时间与电话联络以便照护者出门休息

背景：虚构家庭照护场景中，成年兄妹协调陪伴父亲的时间，不涉及治疗决定。

人物：Nora、Felix。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-13-s01 · Nora: Could you relieve me for an hour? I have been here with Dad all afternoon.
- p09-a-13-s02 · Felix: Of course. I can stay until seven. Have you had anything to eat?
- p09-a-13-s03 · Nora: Yes, he and I had soup together. I just need some fresh air.
- p09-a-13-s04 · Felix: I am relieved to hear that. You sounded as though you had not stopped all day.
- p09-a-13-s05 · Nora: It is a relief to know I can rely on you. I will be back by six thirty.
- p09-a-13-s06 · Felix: Go and enjoy the walk. Keep your phone with you, and I will call if either of us needs you.
- p09-a-13-s07 · Nora: Thanks. It is five thirty now, so that gives me the hour. I'll keep the phone on.

目标用法：

- relief · n. · 宽慰；安心；义项 sense-staged-ox-8f6f4c05844166b3-p09-a-13；来源 ox-8f6f4c05844166b3；证据 p09-a-13-s05；词形 relief。
- relieve · v. · 接替某人工作以让其休息；义项 sense-staged-ox-c771f8a0cb0aacab-p09-a-13；来源 ox-c771f8a0cb0aacab；证据 p09-a-13-s01；词形 relieve。
- relieved · adj. · 感到宽慰的；义项 sense-staged-ox-faed6d004eda2406-p09-a-13；来源 ox-faed6d004eda2406；证据 p09-a-13-s04；词形 relieved。
- rely · v. · 依靠；信赖；义项 sense-staged-ox-9ec8d7f5ca358d36-p09-a-13；来源 ox-9ec8d7f5ca358d36；证据 p09-a-13-s05；词形 rely。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

兄妹协调照护者散步休息，五点半至六点半恰为一小时，接替者能留到七点，时间无冲突。标题和目标准确写愿意接替、回来时间和电话联络，未声称已经完成一小时照护。

## 用具体服务事例准备志愿者提名 · p09-a-14

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：志愿者表彰提名的证据（volunteer-award-nomination-evidence）。

沟通目标：以具体服务记录支持提名，说明奖项不含现金并分工准备提名文字和核对事实

背景：社区组织者准备年度志愿者表彰提名。

人物：Clara、Yusuf。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-14-s01 · Clara: Ana deserves recognition for keeping the reading club going all winter.
- p09-a-14-s02 · Yusuf: She has a remarkable ability to make new readers feel welcome. Can we give an example?
- p09-a-14-s03 · Clara: She made large-print copies for three sessions and arranged lifts for people who could not walk here.
- p09-a-14-s04 · Yusuf: That is stronger than simply saying she has a good reputation. What is the reward this year?
- p09-a-14-s05 · Clara: A certificate and a tree planted in her name, not a cash prize.
- p09-a-14-s06 · Yusuf: Then I'll draft the nomination around those two examples.
- p09-a-14-s07 · Clara: Good. I'll check the dates in the club records before we submit it.

目标用法：

- recognition · n. · 对贡献的认可；表彰；义项 sense-staged-ox-2b933eb82f27e1d1-p09-a-14；来源 ox-2b933eb82f27e1d1；证据 p09-a-14-s01；词形 recognition。

- reward · n. · 对贡献的奖赏；义项 sense-staged-ox-20cc474f1350c5a7-p09-a-14；来源 ox-20cc474f1350c5a7；证据 p09-a-14-s04；词形 reward。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

具体的大字材料及接送例子替代空泛夸奖，说明非现金奖赏后分工起草提名和核对日期。标题及目标都是准备与分工，不把尚未提交的提名写成已获奖，Ana的贡献与待核记录区分清楚。

## 暂缓虚构丑闻标题并安排核实 · p09-a-15

主题：社会与公共事务；情景：虚构资助指控的报道核查（fictional-news-grant-allegation-check）。

沟通目标：区分未证实的指控和已知资料，约定索取付款证据与当事方回应后再决定报道措辞

背景：虚构新闻编辑练习中，两人处理关于艺术资助的匿名指控，内容不是现实新闻。

人物：Grace、Leon。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-15-s01 · Grace: This anonymous message calls the missing arts grant a scandal. Shall that be our headline?
- p09-a-15-s02 · Leon: Not yet. Careful reporting means checking whether the money is actually missing.
- p09-a-15-s03 · Grace: The amount in the message is shocking, but there is no document attached.
- p09-a-15-s04 · Leon: Ask the sender what the records reveal and whether we can see those records ourselves.
- p09-a-15-s05 · Grace: Should I also contact a representative of the arts centre?
- p09-a-15-s06 · Leon: Yes. Ask about the payment schedule and give them the specific claim to answer.
- p09-a-15-s07 · Grace: I'll request the records and their response. Until then, I'll leave that headline out.
- p09-a-15-s08 · Leon: Good. An allegation in a message is not a finding we have verified.

目标用法：

- reporting · n. · 新闻报道工作；义项 sense-staged-ox-b28471992c1dd590-p09-a-15；来源 ox-b28471992c1dd590；证据 p09-a-15-s02；词形 reporting。
- representative · n. · 机构代表；义项 sense-staged-ox-329b7c27decafd86-p09-a-15；来源 ox-329b7c27decafd86；证据 p09-a-15-s05；词形 representative。

- scandal · n. · 丑闻；义项 sense-staged-ox-1fc43e0bd0f99432-p09-a-15；来源 ox-1fc43e0bd0f99432；证据 p09-a-15-s01；词形 scandal。
- shocking · adj. · 令人震惊的；义项 sense-staged-ox-b0f6344b97e426a8-p09-a-15；来源 ox-b0f6344b97e426a8；证据 p09-a-15-s03；词形 shocking。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

虚构新闻练习把匿名指控、惊人金额和未获文件与已核实事实严格区分，决定暂不使用丑闻标题，索证并给机构回应机会。目标是安排核实而非已取得记录或回应，结尾保留未验证状态。

## 向保护区报告近岸受伤鸟并等候 · p09-a-16

主题：自然与环境；情景：海岸保护区受伤鸟求助（coastal-reserve-bird-rescue-call）。

沟通目标：说明受伤鸟的位置，获得保护区派员处理的承诺并决定留在步道上等候

背景：游客致电虚构海岸保护区的工作人员报告可见的受伤鸟。

人物：Ellie、Warden。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-16-s01 · Ellie: I am in the coastal reserve, near the wooden viewing platform. There is a bird dragging one wing.
- p09-a-16-s02 · Warden: Is it on the shore or farther out in the water?
- p09-a-16-s03 · Ellie: In the shallow water beside the red marker, about two metres from the shore.
- p09-a-16-s04 · Warden: Thank you. Our rescue team is nearby. I will send them to the platform.
- p09-a-16-s05 · Ellie: Should I go closer so I can show them?
- p09-a-16-s06 · Warden: Please stay on the path. The red marker gives us a clear location, and the team will meet you there.
- p09-a-16-s07 · Ellie: Understood. I'll wait on the path by the platform and point it out when they arrive.

## 约定俱乐部秘书辞职日期和交接清单 · p09-a-17

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：俱乐部秘书退休搬家交接（club-secretary-retirement-handover）。

沟通目标：明确秘书将在月底辞职，约定先列出交接清单再寻找接任者

背景：虚构社区俱乐部中，现任秘书与主席商量离任交接，尚未向全体成员发送通知。

人物：Arthur、Bea。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-17-s01 · Arthur: My retirement starts next month, and I will be moving closer to my daughter.
- p09-a-17-s02 · Bea: Does that mean you want to leave the club committee as well?
- p09-a-17-s03 · Arthur: Yes. I need to resign as secretary at the end of this month.
- p09-a-17-s04 · Bea: Thank you for telling me early. Could you list the monthly tasks and where the records are kept?
- p09-a-17-s05 · Arthur: Certainly. I'll have a handover list ready by Friday, but I don't know anyone who could take over.
- p09-a-17-s06 · Bea: That is fine. Let's use your list to explain the role before we ask for volunteers.
- p09-a-17-s07 · Arthur: Agreed. I will stay responsible for the records until the last day of the month.

## 调换预约并解决共用录音室冲突 · p09-a-18

主题：工作与协作；情景：共用录音室重复预约处理（shared-studio-booking-dispute-resolution）。

沟通目标：查明两份日历造成的冲突，调换预约时段并在共用日历中确认更新

背景：两位同事发现录音室被同时安排了两项工作。

人物：Zoe、Ian。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-18-s01 · Zoe: We both have the studio at two. How can we resolve this without cancelling either recording?
- p09-a-18-s02 · Ian: I booked on the wall calendar. You used the online one, so we never saw each other.
- p09-a-18-s03 · Zoe: My guest can come at four instead. Would you be able to finish by then?
- p09-a-18-s04 · Ian: Yes. If you take four to five and I keep two to three, that would settle it.
- p09-a-18-s05 · Zoe: That works for my guest; she has already offered four as an alternative. I'll change my booking now.
- p09-a-18-s06 · Ian: I am looking at the online calendar. Yes, yours is now at four, and mine is at two.
- p09-a-18-s07 · Zoe: Great. I will mark the wall calendar as out of use so that we both use the online one from now on.

- resolve · v. · 解决问题或冲突；义项 sense-staged-ox-51e799b9cc454e0a-p09-a-18；来源 ox-51e799b9cc454e0a；证据 p09-a-18-s01；词形 resolve。
- settle · v. · 解决争议；使问题得到处理；义项 sense-staged-ox-18a0e804596d7add-p09-a-18；来源 ox-18a0e804596d7add；证据 p09-a-18-s04；词形 settle。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

## 核算住宿交通估价并约定索取费用明细 · p09-a-19

主题：出行与住宿；情景：乡间度假村费用比较（rural-resort-cost-comparison）。

沟通目标：把交通加入两处住宿估价，确认可能节省约三十英镑并决定先索取完整报价再预订

背景：朋友为虚构周末行程比较宣传单价格，所有金额仅用于对话练习。

人物：Elena、Max。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-19-s01 · Elena: The rural resort is two hundred and forty pounds for two nights. The hotel in town is three hundred and thirty.
- p09-a-19-s02 · Max: The resort has no station pickup. A return taxi would be roughly sixty pounds, according to this estimate.
- p09-a-19-s03 · Elena: So the total would be about three hundred, not two hundred and forty.
- p09-a-19-s04 · Max: Right. The hotel includes its station shuttle, so the resort would offer a saving of only about thirty pounds.
- p09-a-19-s05 · Elena: And we have not checked whether breakfast costs extra at the resort.
- p09-a-19-s06 · Max: Shall we ask for a full price, including breakfast, before booking either one?
- p09-a-19-s07 · Elena: Yes. I'll ask for that breakdown. Thirty pounds is only a possible difference until we know all the charges.

## 限定虚构日记展签的解释并约定核对 · p09-a-20

主题：学习与技能；情景：虚构定居者日记展签修订安排（revolution-exhibit-caption-evidence）。

沟通目标：约定在草稿中保留定居者抵达日期、删去日记解释革命起因的无依据说法，并在印刷前请学者核对修订稿

背景：博物馆写作课堂使用虚构地区、日记与革命材料练习展签编辑，并非真实历史陈述。

人物：Pavel、Ruth。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-20-s01 · Pavel: Our caption says this diary explains why the revolution began. Does the source card support that?
- p09-a-20-s02 · Ruth: No. It only identifies the writer as a settler who arrived ten years before it happened.
- p09-a-20-s03 · Pavel: Then we are giving the diary a significance that the evidence does not establish.
- p09-a-20-s04 · Ruth: We should retain the arrival date, but remove the claim about the cause of the revolution.
- p09-a-20-s05 · Pavel: Would this revision work: "This diary records one family's first winter in the region"?
- p09-a-20-s06 · Ruth: That matches the extract. The visiting scholar can check the wording against the full diary.
- p09-a-20-s07 · Pavel: Agreed. I'll keep the date and use that narrower sentence in the draft, then ask her to check it before we print anything.

- p09-a-21-s03 · Kian: The song on the blue page. Could we select that one for today?

- p09-a-22-s03 · Jules: I do not want to rush this just because the van is here.
- p09-a-22-s04 · Amir: Neither do I. The fitted box will be ready tomorrow. We should move the model then.
- p09-a-22-s05 · Jules: Agreed. I'll tell the gallery the delivery will be a day late and explain why.

## 调整虚构劫案短剧的声音提示 · p09-a-23

主题：文娱与运动；情景：虚构劫案戏剧声效排练（fictional-robbery-scene-sound-cue）。

沟通目标：用低音量台外呼喊提示劫案情节，约定演员听到呼喊后转身而不用突发巨响

背景：成年人排练虚构舞台短剧，只讨论叙事声音与表演提示，不演练真实犯罪。

人物：Rosa、Dylan。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-23-s01 · Rosa: The script says two characters rob a shop, but the audience never sees the shop. How will they know what happened?
- p09-a-23-s02 · Dylan: The next line mentions the robbery. Before that, a scream comes from offstage.
- p09-a-23-s03 · Rosa: The sound recording is so loud that it will scare people rather than help them follow the story.
- p09-a-23-s04 · Dylan: Then use a brief live cry at a lower volume. No sudden bang.
- p09-a-23-s05 · Rosa: And I turn toward the side door when I hear it?
- p09-a-23-s06 · Dylan: Yes. Wait for the cry, turn, and then deliver your line.
- p09-a-23-s07 · Rosa: All right. Let us practise from the moment before the cry, with that quieter cue.

## 约定先请店员查看玫瑰根系再选盆 · p09-a-25

主题：居家生活；情景：玫瑰盆栽根系查看请求（rose-pot-position-choice）。

沟通目标：描述盆底根系和阳台光照，决定请工作人员查看后再选购花盆

背景：朋友在园艺店入口查看带来的盆栽与陈列花盆，尚未得到园艺人员意见。

人物：Demi、Ash。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-25-s01 · Demi: There is a root sticking out through the hole under this rose. Does it need a bigger pot?
- p09-a-25-s02 · Ash: I do not know. Let us ask the staff to look before we buy one.
- p09-a-25-s03 · Demi: These specially shaped pots would fit along my narrow balcony.
- p09-a-25-s04 · Ash: Would they leave the plant in the same position?
- p09-a-25-s05 · Demi: No. There is much more shade along that wall than where it sits now.
- p09-a-25-s06 · Ash: Then mention that as well. A pot that fits the space may not solve the problem.
- p09-a-25-s07 · Demi: Good point. I'll show the staff the root and explain the light before choosing a pot.

盆底露根、狭窄阳台及新位置更阴形成同一选盆任务，朋友不猜测处理方法而决定让店员先看。标题和目标均准确为约定或决定请求查看，未宣称店员已经检查、推荐或售出花盆。

## 发现新桌面划痕后约定店方查看 · p09-a-26

- p09-a-26-s04 · Anton: Have you tried to rub the mark with anything?
- p09-a-26-s05 · Mabel: Only a dry cloth, very lightly. It did not change.
- p09-a-26-s06 · Anton: Please leave it as it is and send us a clear photo. I will arrange for our service team to inspect it.
- p09-a-26-s07 · Mabel: All right. I'll stop wiping it and send photos of the mark and the whole table.

## 确定太空展图像顺序与比例说明 · p09-a-27

主题：学习与技能；情景：太空模型展图卡顺序与比例说明安排（space-exhibit-photo-sequence）。

沟通目标：依据给定图卡确定发射与入轨图像的顺序、选定清晰照片，并约定重排图卡及补充非等比例展示说明

背景：学生用虚构教学图卡布置太空模型展示，核对的是图卡所示顺序。

人物：Hugo、Zara。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-27-s01 · Hugo: Our sequence starts with the satellite already in orbit, then shows the launch. Is that deliberate?
- p09-a-27-s02 · Zara: No, I mixed up the cards. The rocket on the launch pad should come first.
- p09-a-27-s03 · Hugo: Then the launch, followed by the card showing the satellite in orbit, as the numbered guide says.
- p09-a-27-s04 · Zara: Yes. Which launch photo should we use?
- p09-a-27-s05 · Hugo: This shot is clearer. You can see the whole rocket rather than only a cloud of smoke.
- p09-a-27-s06 · Zara: Good choice. We also need to say these drawings are not to scale. The model Earth and the satellite look almost the same size.
- p09-a-27-s07 · Hugo: Agreed. I'll use this photo, put the cards in that order, and add the scale note below them.

## 核对定制架子后签收 · p09-a-28

主题：消费与服务；情景：定制架子送货验收（custom-shelf-delivery-signoff）。

人物：April、Lewis。

- p09-a-28-s01 · April: Before I sign, can we check that the shelf is exactly eighty centimetres wide?
- p09-a-28-s02 · Lewis: Here is the tape measure. It shows eighty, as ordered.
- p09-a-28-s03 · April: And the cupboard door opens fully beside it. That was my other concern.
- p09-a-28-s04 · Lewis: Does it satisfy the requirements we agreed on?
- p09-a-28-s05 · April: Yes. I am satisfied with the size and the finish.
- p09-a-28-s06 · Lewis: Then I need your signature on this delivery inspection form, beside today's date.
- p09-a-28-s07 · April: Here you are. I have signed it after the width check.

## 询问转行课程资助并找到资格清单入口 · p09-a-29

主题：学习与技能；情景：转行学习的资助条件询问（career-course-scholarship-inquiry）。

沟通目标：说明求职和转行目标，了解资助并非自动获批且需核对条件，并找到资格清单的查看入口

背景：虚构成人教育咨询练习中，求职者询问课程资助，不构成真实资助资格或财务建议。

人物：Noel、Farah。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-29-s01 · Noel: I am a job seeker hoping to move into the arts sector. Is this course only for people who already work there?
- p09-a-29-s02 · Farah: No. It is designed for people changing careers as well.
- p09-a-29-s03 · Noel: I want to seek work in a gallery, but I cannot afford the full fee. Is there any help?
- p09-a-29-s04 · Farah: There is a scholarship scheme. It covers the course fee for some applicants, but places are limited.
- p09-a-29-s05 · Noel: Does being unemployed mean I automatically qualify for a scholarship?
- p09-a-29-s06 · Farah: No. You need to check the full conditions and submit an application. The eligibility checklist is linked at the bottom of the course page.
- p09-a-29-s07 · Noel: I can see the link. I'll read the checklist first rather than assume the fee will be covered.

## 修正虚构参议院提案的表决状态 · p09-a-30

主题：学习与技能；情景：虚构参议院提案与通过状态（fictional-senate-resolution-classification）。

沟通目标：区分议员提交提案和参议院通过决议，将课堂图表状态改为等待表决

背景：公民教育课堂使用完全虚构的参议院卡片练习机构与程序用语，不涉及现实政治新闻。

人物：Dara、Colin。

- p09-a-30-s01 · Dara: This card says the senator has submitted a resolution asking for longer library hours.
- p09-a-30-s02 · Colin: So can I write that the senate has approved the change?
- p09-a-30-s03 · Dara: No. The next card says the vote is scheduled for Thursday. One member has proposed it, but the chamber has not decided.
- p09-a-30-s04 · Colin: Then the status should be "awaiting a vote," not "approved."
- p09-a-30-s05 · Dara: Exactly. We can show the proposal reaching the chamber without drawing an arrow to implementation.
- p09-a-30-s06 · Colin: I have changed the status box to "awaiting a vote" and removed that final arrow.
- p09-a-30-s07 · Dara: That now matches the cards. Leave the result open until the exercise gives us the vote.

## 说明临时安置点毛毯短缺并请求库存核对 · p09-a-31

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：风暴安置点物资短缺沟通（storm-shelter-blanket-shortage）。

沟通目标：说明场地受损与安置点毛毯缺口，约定向仓库询问库存后回报是否能够供货

背景：虚构应急沟通练习中，两位社区工作人员协调风暴后的物资，不提供真实灾情或现场救援指令。

人物：Imani、Peter。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-31-s01 · Imani: The severe storm has forced more families to leave their homes. Can we use the school hall?
- p09-a-31-s02 · Peter: No. The roof was severely damaged. The sports centre is the temporary shelter listed on our update.
- p09-a-31-s03 · Imani: The team there reports a shortage of blankets. They have twelve, but they need twenty.
- p09-a-31-s04 · Peter: So they are asking for eight more. I can check the supply warehouse.
- p09-a-31-s05 · Imani: Please do, but do not promise a delivery before you know what is available.
- p09-a-31-s06 · Peter: Understood. I'll ask about stock and transport, then call you with a confirmed answer.
- p09-a-31-s07 · Imani: Thanks. I'll tell the shelter team we are checking, not that the blankets are already on the way.

目标用法：

- severe · adj. · 严重的；猛烈的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ac101fb143b02f97-p09-a-31；来源 ox-ac101fb143b02f97；证据 p09-a-31-s01；词形 severe。
- severely · adv. · 严重地；义项 sense-staged-ox-f1617cd125eb4338-p09-a-31；来源 ox-f1617cd125eb4338；证据 p09-a-31-s02；词形 severely。
- shortage · n. · 短缺；义项 sense-staged-ox-5c2525959c48ed16-p09-a-31；来源 ox-5c2525959c48ed16；证据 p09-a-31-s03；词形 shortage。
- shelter · n. · 临时庇护场所；安置点；义项 sense-staged-ox-c72ebc933a3f555f-p09-a-31；来源 ox-c72ebc933a3f555f；证据 p09-a-31-s02；词形 shelter。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

受损的是学校礼堂屋顶，实际安置点改为体育中心，毛毯二十减十二缺八条算术一致。标题是向同事请求查库存，s05有明确请求；目标准确为约定查库存和运输后回报，结尾特意不说已发货。

## 为街边拍摄选定不挡通行的机位 · p09-a-32

主题：工作与协作；情景：街边外景拍摄光线与通道确认（film-sidewalk-lighting-position）。

沟通目标：核对演员即将到场及开拍安排，选定阴影较少且不占人行道的拍摄位置

背景：虚构摄制组在获准使用的店铺前院内准备外景，保持公共通道畅通。

人物：Nico、Tara。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-32-s01 · Nico: The actors will be here shortly. Is everything ready for today's shooting?
- p09-a-32-s02 · Tara: Almost. That tree casts a shadow over the doorway, so their faces will be dark.
- p09-a-32-s03 · Nico: Could we move the camera onto the sidewalk and film from there?
- p09-a-32-s04 · Tara: No, that would block people. Our marked filming area is inside the forecourt.
- p09-a-32-s05 · Nico: What about this corner, still inside the marks? The doorway looks brighter from here.
- p09-a-32-s06 · Tara: Yes, the doorway is clearly lit on the monitor. Let us use that position and keep the public path clear.
- p09-a-32-s07 · Nico: Agreed. I'll secure the camera here, then we can begin once the actors have rehearsed the entrance.

- sidewalk · n. · 人行道；义项 sense-staged-ox-fa3ed382b9870b21-p09-a-32；来源 ox-fa3ed382b9870b21；证据 p09-a-32-s03；词形 sidewalk。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

<a id="dialogue-p09-a-33"></a>

## 不把未回复当拒绝并约定简短询问 · p09-a-33

主题：家庭与人际；情景：兄弟姐妹探访未回复的理解（sibling-visit-message-interpretation）。

沟通目标：承认未能确认探访的遗憾，不把沉默解读为拒绝，并决定发一条不催促的询问信息

背景：朋友讨论一位成年家人迟迟未回复探访安排的情况。

人物：Robin、Jae。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-33-s01 · Robin: Alex is my only sibling, and I really wanted to visit this weekend. There is still no reply.
- p09-a-33-s02 · Jae: It is a shame you cannot make a plan yet. How long has it been?
- p09-a-33-s03 · Robin: Two days. I keep reading the silence as a sign that Alex does not want me there.
- p09-a-33-s04 · Jae: It could mean many things. Have you sent a simple question about whether this weekend works?
- p09-a-33-s05 · Robin: Not really. My message was a long list of possible plans.
- p09-a-33-s06 · Jae: You could ask once, without asking for an immediate answer, and leave the weekend open for now.
- p09-a-33-s07 · Robin: I'll do that: one short message asking whether a visit would work, and no more planning until I hear back.

目标用法：

- sibling · n. · 兄弟或姐妹；义项 sense-staged-ox-0d09982b5f7b7bc6-p09-a-33；来源 ox-0d09982b5f7b7bc6；证据 p09-a-33-s01；词形 sibling。
- shame · n. · 令人遗憾的事；义项 sense-staged-ox-25f7328312e64679-p09-a-33；来源 ox-25f7328312e64679；证据 p09-a-33-s02；词形 shame。
- silence · n. · 没有回应；沉默；义项 sense-staged-ox-0c0c50332cf81be4-p09-a-33；来源 ox-0c0c50332cf81be4；证据 p09-a-33-s03；词形 silence。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

兄弟姐妹两天未回复杂探访信息造成失落，朋友拒绝把无回应等同拒绝，最终决定发一次简短不催促的询问并暂不安排。标题与目标准确是理解和约定，未说对方已回复或同意探访。

## 听朋友诉说咖啡馆停业并约定陪同取物 · p09-a-34

主题：家庭与人际；情景：突发店铺关闭后的陪伴（unexpected-cafe-closure-support）。

沟通目标：听取朋友因家庭咖啡馆突然停业而震惊的感受，约定周六陪同取回个人物品

背景：朋友谈论虚构家庭咖啡馆停业带来的失落，没有真实商业指控或法律意见。

人物：Lena、Owen。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-34-s01 · Lena: My parents closed the cafe yesterday. They had been considering it, but I thought we had another month.
- p09-a-34-s02 · Owen: That must have been a shock, especially after spending so much time there.
- p09-a-34-s03 · Lena: I am still shocked by how quickly it happened. My sketchbooks are all in the back room.
- p09-a-34-s04 · Owen: Would you like company when you collect them? We do not have to talk about the closure if you do not want to.
- p09-a-34-s05 · Lena: Yes, please. My parents will let me in on Saturday morning.
- p09-a-34-s06 · Owen: Then I'll meet you outside at ten on Saturday and help carry your things.
- p09-a-34-s07 · Lena: Thank you. Ten works, and having someone there will make it easier.

目标用法：

- shocked · adj. · 感到震惊的；义项 sense-staged-ox-260b7259898f2b62-p09-a-34；来源 ox-260b7259898f2b62；证据 p09-a-34-s03；词形 shocked。
- shock · n. · 令人震惊的经历或感受；义项 sense-staged-ox-993e87e399a6d985-p09-a-34；来源 ox-993e87e399a6d985；证据 p09-a-34-s02；词形 shock。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

家中咖啡馆比预期早停业引起震惊，朋友提出不强迫讨论、陪同取素描本的具体帮助，双方确认周六十点。标题及目标均保留陪同取物的约定状态，未把感受推为法律指控或宣称物品已取回。

绑定文件 SHA-256：b6eeaabc922eb9ce41167a780d85ffb14f3a3d14a65eba704b06b59bdf383b49

- ox-260b7259898f2b62：accepted；s03中am still shocked by how quickly...以情绪形容词作表语，描述Lena对骤然停业速度的震惊。（p09-a-34-s03）。
- ox-993e87e399a6d985：accepted；s02中must have been a shock为名词表示这件事带来的震惊经历，与s03个人感受相呼应而非医学休克。（p09-a-34-s02）。

<a id="dialogue-p09-a-35"></a>

## 反馈研讨课提速并约定增加提问停顿 · p09-a-35

主题：学习与技能；情景：研讨课节奏反馈与调整（seminar-session-pace-feedback）。

沟通目标：说明本周课程节奏明显加快及练习不足，约定下次课每个例子后留出提问时间

背景：成人课程学员向讲师反馈本周研讨课节奏。

人物：Sana、Victor。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-35-s01 · Sana: Could we talk about the seminar? This week felt significantly faster than the first one.
- p09-a-35-s02 · Victor: I covered more examples, so that is useful to hear. Where did you lose track?
- p09-a-35-s03 · Sana: After the second example. There was no time to try the method before you moved on.
- p09-a-35-s04 · Victor: That is a significant change from last week, when we paused after each example. I should not have removed all those pauses.
- p09-a-35-s05 · Sana: Could we have a moment for questions in the next session, even if it means fewer examples?
- p09-a-35-s06 · Victor: Yes. I will pause after each example, let you try the step, and take questions before moving on.
- p09-a-35-s07 · Sana: That sounds better. I would rather understand two examples than rush through four.

目标用法：

- seminar · n. · 研讨课；义项 sense-staged-ox-b8c688034428d024-p09-a-35；来源 ox-b8c688034428d024；证据 p09-a-35-s01；词形 seminar。
- session · n. · 一节课程；一次活动；义项 sense-staged-ox-9017846dd9c946d1-p09-a-35；来源 ox-9017846dd9c946d1；证据 p09-a-35-s05；词形 session。
- significant · adj. · 显著的；重要的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c61350d4e01f45d2-p09-a-35；来源 ox-c61350d4e01f45d2；证据 p09-a-35-s04；词形 significant。
- significantly · adv. · 显著地；明显地；义项 sense-staged-ox-5898dc8c15fd0015-p09-a-35；来源 ox-5898dc8c15fd0015；证据 p09-a-35-s01；词形 significantly。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

学员具体指出第二个例子后缺少试做时间，教师承认移除停顿的问题，约定下次每例后练习并提问。标题和目标准确区分本周提速反馈与下次课程调整承诺，不误称教学改善已实施。

## 核对材质后选定便于旅行的真丝围巾 · p09-a-36

主题：消费与服务；情景：旅行围巾材质与体积选择（silk-scarf-shade-choice）。

沟通目标：查看材质标签并比较收纳体积，选定较薄的真丝围巾作为旅行礼物

背景：顾客为经常出行的成年朋友挑选轻便围巾。

人物：Dina、Shop assistant。

起草：author-phase09-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-a-36-s01 · Dina: This scarf feels very light. Is it silk, or a fabric that just looks like it?
- p09-a-36-s02 · Shop assistant: The label says one hundred percent silk. This other scarf is wool and takes up more space.
- p09-a-36-s03 · Dina: The gift is for a friend who travels with one small bag. Could you fold both so I can compare them?
- p09-a-36-s04 · Shop assistant: Of course. Here they are side by side.
- p09-a-36-s05 · Dina: The thinner one will fit more easily. I will take the silk scarf, please.
- p09-a-36-s06 · Shop assistant: Certainly. Would you like it in a small gift envelope rather than a box?
- p09-a-36-s07 · Dina: Yes, that would be perfect for the bag.

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-a；整篇结论：accepted。

店员读出全真丝标签并将两条围巾折好供比较，顾客选定薄款真丝围巾并接受小礼封包装。标题和目标都是核对材质与选定旅行礼物，不声称付款或交付已完成。

<a id="dialogue-p09-b-01"></a>

## 约好为丧亲同事代领午餐 · p09-b-01

主题：家庭与人际；情景：约好为丧亲同事代领午餐（bereaved-colleague-support-choice）。

沟通目标：听取丧亲同事对慰问方式的感受，并约定代领午餐而不转发私人消息

背景：虚构办公室场景：同事失去亲人后返回工作岗位，两人讨论怎样提供他真正需要的支持。

人物：Marta、Elias。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-01-s01 · Marta: I'm terribly sorry about your brother, Elias. Would you rather I left you alone today?
- p09-b-01-s02 · Elias: No, stay. It's been a tough week since he died, but being on my own isn't helping.
- p09-b-01-s03 · Marta: People have been asking me to pass on their sympathy. I haven't shared your message with anyone.
- p09-b-01-s04 · Elias: Thank you. I know their concern is sincere. I just can't face answering twenty messages.
- p09-b-01-s05 · Marta: Would it help if I collected your lunch? I know that won't take away your suffering.
- p09-b-01-s06 · Elias: It would. You've been sympathetic without making me explain everything.
- p09-b-01-s07 · Marta: Then I'll pick up the sandwich you ordered. And your message stays between us.
- p09-b-01-s08 · Elias: Yes, please. That's all I need for now.

目标用法：

- terribly · adv. · 非常；极其；义项 sense-staged-ox-01a71ca11e7ad10a-p09-b-01；来源 ox-01a71ca11e7ad10a；证据 p09-b-01-s01；词形 terribly。

## 约好先检查金属椅子再报价 · p09-b-02

主题：消费与服务；情景：约好先检查金属椅子再报价（metal-chair-specialist-inspection）。

- p09-b-02-s01 · Jonah: The frame is steel, but the seat folds up. Can your shop repair this kind of chair?

- p09-b-02-s03 · Jonah: The folding joint is stiff. I'm worried I'll break it if I force it.
- p09-b-02-s04 · Farah: Leave it as it is. Our repairer is skilled at working on old joints, but she'll need to see it first.
- p09-b-02-s05 · Jonah: Can I bring it in tomorrow and get a price before any work starts?
- p09-b-02-s06 · Farah: Certainly. Bring it after ten. We'll inspect it, then call you with a quote.
- p09-b-02-s07 · Jonah: Good. Please don't start the repair until I've agreed to that quote.

- steel · n. · 钢；义项 sense-staged-ox-d712974fdc145d5a-p09-b-02；来源 ox-d712974fdc145d5a；证据 p09-b-02-s01；词形 steel。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Jonah describes a stiff folding-chair joint, and Farah schedules inspection after ten followed by a telephone quote. The final request reserves authorization for repairs; no repair or price is falsely reported as completed. The title and goal accurately name the metal chair and agreed inspection-before-quote sequence.

- p09-b-03-s05 · Lina: I'll change the model label to 'skull' and copy the photograph's caption exactly.

## 分工处理咖啡馆洒落的饮料 · p09-b-04

- p09-b-04-s06 · Leena: Yes. I'll keep the barrier there until the floor is dry.
- p09-b-04-s07 · Nico: Thanks. I'll check it with you before we let customers through again.

## 约定按实际幅度描述销售趋势 · p09-b-05

主题：工作与协作；情景：约定按实际幅度描述销售趋势（weekly-sales-trend-wording）。

沟通目标：核对每周销售图的变化，并约定区分先前平稳期与近期的小幅下降

背景：同事检查一张虚构商店销售图，避免把轻微变化夸大为崩跌。

- p09-b-05-s01 · Asha: I've described April sales as a collapse. Does that sound too strong?
- p09-b-05-s02 · Bram: Much too strong. The line shows a slight fall in the final week, not a collapse.
- p09-b-05-s03 · Asha: What about the first three weeks? The figures aren't exactly identical.
- p09-b-05-s04 · Bram: They're close enough to call sales steady during that period. Look at March: sales rose steadily every week then.
- p09-b-05-s05 · Asha: So April ended somewhat below where it began, after three fairly stable weeks.
- p09-b-05-s06 · Bram: Exactly. Keep March separate so readers don't think the upward trend continued all through April.
- p09-b-05-s07 · Asha: I'll replace 'collapse' with that more precise description and label the two months clearly.

- slight · adj. · 轻微的；少量的；义项 sense-staged-ox-615213a14ba5da2c-p09-b-05；来源 ox-615213a14ba5da2c；证据 p09-b-05-s02；词形 slight。

- somewhat · adv. · 有些；稍微；义项 sense-staged-ox-778bd427c63277f5-p09-b-05；来源 ox-778bd427c63277f5；证据 p09-b-05-s05；词形 somewhat。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The colleagues replace an exaggerated collapse claim with a small late-April fall after a near-steady period, separately identifying March’s rise. s07 commits to revising wording and month labels without pretending the edit is complete. The Chinese title and goal match this evidence-bounded wording agreement.

- ox-57859ffa6852b95a：accepted；s04 calls the close first-three-week figures steady, with exactly identical explicitly unnecessary in s03. The adjective means 稳定的 in this bounded interval.（p09-b-05-s04）。
- ox-6db83dc58c7bf30f：accepted；s04 uses rose steadily every week for March. The adverb modifies rose, conveying a continuing regular increase and matching 持续而稳定地.（p09-b-05-s04）。

## 决定只补进售罄的蓝色杯子 · p09-b-06

主题：工作与协作；情景：决定只补进售罄的蓝色杯子（market-stall-restock-limit）。

沟通目标：核对市集摊位的存货与补货资金，并决定只补进售罄的蓝色杯子

背景：市集摊主与搭档在收摊后安排下一次摆摊的存货。

人物：Otis、Mei。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-06-s01 · Otis: Before we pack up the stall, can we check what we need for next Saturday?
- p09-b-06-s02 · Mei: We've sold all the blue mugs, but there's plenty of stock left in the other colours.
- p09-b-06-s03 · Otis: I'd like to order another box of every colour.
- p09-b-06-s04 · Mei: We've set aside a sum of eighty pounds for restocking. That won't cover four boxes.
- p09-b-06-s05 · Otis: How much is one box of blue mugs?
- p09-b-06-s06 · Mei: Fifty pounds, including delivery. And we already have enough red, green and white ones for another market.
- p09-b-06-s07 · Otis: Then let's order just the blue ones and keep the remaining thirty pounds for now.
- p09-b-06-s08 · Mei: Agreed. I'll place that order tomorrow.

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The stallholders check unsold colours and an eighty-pound budget, then agree to spend fifty pounds only on sold-out blue mugs and retain thirty. Mei will order tomorrow, so the title and goal correctly report a decision rather than a placed order. The numerical and inventory details support the choice.

## 约定缩小咖啡馆海报的环保宣传范围 · p09-b-07

主题：工作与协作；情景：约定缩小咖啡馆海报的环保宣传范围（solar-promotion-claim-scope）。

沟通目标：确认整体环保宣传缺乏依据，并约定删去该宣传、只保留已核实的太阳能热水信息

背景：文案人员与咖啡馆经理检查一张虚构门店海报，不对真实产品作环保认证。

人物：Selma、Ravi。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-07-s01 · Selma: The new slogan is 'A completely sustainable café'. Can we support that?
- p09-b-07-s02 · Ravi: We've installed solar panels to heat water, but we haven't assessed our deliveries or packaging.
- p09-b-07-s03 · Selma: Then 'completely' is a problem. I don't want us to become another so-called green business that can't explain its claims.
- p09-b-07-s04 · Ravi: Could we just say we use solar energy to heat water? The installation report confirms that.
- p09-b-07-s05 · Selma: Yes. That describes one specific change without making a claim about the whole café.
- p09-b-07-s06 · Ravi: Let's use that wording and leave out the broader slogan.
- p09-b-07-s07 · Selma: Agreed. I'll make the change before the poster goes to print.

目标用法：

- slogan · n. · 宣传标语；义项 sense-staged-ox-1b6f99e97db98ccf-p09-b-07；来源 ox-1b6f99e97db98ccf；证据 p09-b-07-s01；词形 slogan。
- sustainable · adj. · 可持续的；不破坏环境资源的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5bdfc95074081d52-p09-b-07；来源 ox-5bdfc95074081d52；证据 p09-b-07-s01；词形 sustainable。
- solar · adj. · 太阳能的；义项 sense-staged-ox-84618146ba2edebf-p09-b-07；来源 ox-84618146ba2edebf；证据 p09-b-07-s02、p09-b-07-s04；词形 solar。
- so-called · adj. · 所谓的（表示怀疑其名实相符）；义项 sense-staged-ox-c24e2810df6b8bd8-p09-b-07；来源 ox-c24e2810df6b8bd8；证据 p09-b-07-s03；词形 so-called。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Selma questions an unsupported whole-cafe environmental slogan, while Ravi supplies narrower installation evidence for solar hot water. They agree to replace the broad claim before printing, not certify the business or claim the edit is complete. Both Chinese title and goal accurately describe this proposed narrowing and its evidence.

## 约好周四午间练习困难乐段 · p09-b-08

主题：文娱与运动；情景：约好周四午间练习困难乐段（rehearsal-slot-small-restart）。

- p09-b-08-s03 · Dylan: Could we go through that bit together sometime this week?
- p09-b-08-s04 · Inez: I can spare fifteen minutes on Thursday at lunch. Would that work?
- p09-b-08-s05 · Dylan: Yes. Let's start at twelve thirty and practise only the change into the last verse.
- p09-b-08-s06 · Inez: Deal. Bring your marked copy, and we'll take it slowly.

The rehearsal exchange acknowledges a specific last-verse mistake, gives proportionate encouragement and fixes a fifteen-minute Thursday lunch practice at twelve thirty. - p09-b-10-s04 · Guide Arun: That's fine. The public tour stays in the open courtyard. Joining the ceremony is a separate choice.

- p09-b-11-s03 · Petra: Could we say that trampling can threaten plants along the bank?
- p09-b-11-s04 · Wes: Yes, and connect that directly to what we want people to do: stay on the path.
- p09-b-11-s05 · Petra: I'll mention the trampling damage we recorded, ask visitors to stay on the path, and remove the claim about every species.
- p09-b-11-s06 · Wes: Good. Put the survey date underneath, so the notice is clear about its evidence.

## 约定先查借阅记录再判断档案箱去向 · p09-b-14

主题：工作与协作；情景：约定先查借阅记录再判断档案箱去向（missing-archive-box-evidence）。

沟通目标：区分档案箱不在原位与失窃猜测，并约定先追查借阅记录

背景：档案室同事发现一个箱子不在架上，避免在没有证据时指责他人。

人物：Nadia、Felix。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-14-s01 · Nadia: The photograph box is gone. I suspect someone borrowed it without signing it out.
- p09-b-14-s02 · Felix: Possibly, but let's not speculate about who took it. Have you checked yesterday's loan records?
- p09-b-14-s03 · Nadia: Only the paper sheet. There's nothing on it. Should I report a theft?
- p09-b-14-s04 · Felix: We don't know that anything was stolen. At the moment, that's speculation.
- p09-b-14-s05 · Nadia: Right. Can we trace the box through the electronic requests?
- p09-b-14-s06 · Felix: Yes. I'll check those while you ask the reading-room team whether they have it.
- p09-b-14-s07 · Nadia: Agreed. We'll compare what we find before describing it as stolen.

- speculate · v. · 推测；义项 sense-staged-ox-ace117d14c5fee3b-p09-b-14；来源 ox-ace117d14c5fee3b；证据 p09-b-14-s02；词形 speculate。

- trace · v. · 追查；追踪去向；义项 sense-staged-ox-4c9fbca3d3681866-p09-b-14；来源 ox-4c9fbca3d3681866；证据 p09-b-14-s05；词形 trace。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The archive colleagues separate a box absent from its shelf from an unproved theft claim. They agree to check electronic requests and ask the reading-room team before comparing findings. The Chinese title and goal correctly report a future evidence-gathering plan, not a located box or confirmed theft, and the two assigned searches give a practical ending.

## 决定不给汤加辣并先放凉 · p09-b-15

主题：饮食沟通；情景：决定不给汤加辣并先放凉（soup-spice-and-cooling-choice）。

沟通目标：说明汤的调味和温度感受，并决定不加辣椒、放凉后再喝

人物：Rosie、Ben。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-15-s01 · Rosie: What's the spice in this soup? It smells warm and sweet, but not like chilli.
- p09-b-15-s02 · Ben: The recipe uses cinnamon. There are chilli flakes on the table if you want some heat.
- p09-b-15-s03 · Rosie: No, I like it as it is. There's still a lot of steam coming off it, though.
- p09-b-15-s04 · Ben: I tried a spoonful too soon. It was too hot to swallow comfortably.
- p09-b-15-s05 · Rosie: Let's leave it for a few minutes and eat the bread first. I'm not adding any chilli.
- p09-b-15-s06 · Ben: Same here. The flavour's strong enough already.

- spice · n. · 香料；义项 sense-staged-ox-69a79aa4e0e6450f-p09-b-15；来源 ox-69a79aa4e0e6450f；证据 p09-b-15-s01；词形 spice。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The friends distinguish cinnamon aroma from optional chilli heat, then notice that the soup is physically too hot. Rosie proposes waiting and eating bread first, while both reject extra chilli. The Chinese title and goal correctly describe their choice to let soup cool and avoid additional chilli, not a claim that it has already cooled.

## 为排练中的咒骂道歉并约定改用暂停 · p09-b-16

主题：家庭与人际；情景：为排练中的咒骂道歉并约定改用暂停（rehearsal-insult-apology-boundary）。

沟通目标：听取排练冲突造成的感受，承认咒骂冒犯，并约定以后用暂停请求代替

背景：虚构业余剧团场景：一位成员为排练争执中的言语向队友道歉。

人物：Tomas、Yara。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-16-s01 · Tomas: I'm sorry I shouted at you in rehearsal. There was a lot of tension in the room, but that wasn't your fault.
- p09-b-16-s02 · Yara: It felt as if you wanted to hurt me out of spite because I'd questioned your idea.
- p09-b-16-s03 · Tomas: I was embarrassed and reacted badly. I shouldn't swear at you just because we disagree.
- p09-b-16-s04 · Yara: And when you stare at me without saying anything afterwards, I don't know whether it's safe to speak.
- p09-b-16-s05 · Tomas: I understand. If I need a break, I'll say so instead of shouting or glaring.
- p09-b-16-s06 · Yara: All right. Let's agree that either of us can ask for a pause, without being mocked for it.
- p09-b-16-s07 · Tomas: Agreed. And I'm sorry for what I said, not just for making the rehearsal awkward.

目标用法：

- tension · n. · 紧张气氛；义项 sense-staged-ox-94331809e3ed242a-p09-b-16；来源 ox-94331809e3ed242a；证据 p09-b-16-s01；词形 tension。
- spite · n. · 恶意；怨恨；义项 sense-staged-ox-844d9615f60802d1-p09-b-16；来源 ox-844d9615f60802d1；证据 p09-b-16-s02；词形 spite。
- swear · v. · 咒骂；说脏话；义项 sense-staged-ox-8ddf0e352a64bf07-p09-b-16；来源 ox-8ddf0e352a64bf07；证据 p09-b-16-s03；词形 swear。
- stare · v. · 盯着看；义项 sense-staged-ox-d18bfd018052a03e-p09-b-16；来源 ox-d18bfd018052a03e；证据 p09-b-16-s04；词形 stare。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Tomas apologizes for his conduct rather than blaming the tense rehearsal, and Yara explains the impact of both insults and staring. They explicitly agree that either person may request a pause without mockery. The Chinese title and goal correctly describe the apology and future alternative, without claiming all hurt has disappeared.

- ox-94331809e3ed242a：accepted；s01 a lot of tension in the room refers to interpersonal strain during the rehearsal. The mass noun matches 紧张气氛 rather than physical tautness.（p09-b-16-s01）。
- ox-844d9615f60802d1：accepted；s02 out of spite is the conventional noun phrase for malicious or resentful motivation. It is framed as Yara’s felt interpretation, and 恶意/怨恨 is the right sense, not the unrelated in spite of construction.（p09-b-16-s02）。
- ox-8ddf0e352a64bf07：accepted；s03 should not swear at you uses swear with at for directing offensive language at someone. 咒骂/说脏话 fits the admission of bad conduct; it is not the sense of making an oath.（p09-b-16-s03）。
- ox-d18bfd018052a03e：accepted；s04 stare at me without saying anything uses the intransitive verb with at for a fixed prolonged look. 盯着看 matches Yara’s discomfort and Tomas’s later reference to glaring.（p09-b-16-s04）。

## 决定先要求赞助方取消发言稿审批条件 · p09-b-17

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：决定先要求赞助方取消发言稿审批条件（club-sponsorship-editorial-boundary）。

沟通目标：说明社团对赞助条件的分歧，并决定先要求保留独立发言权而不立即接受资助

人物：Nell、Karim。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-17-s01 · Nell: There's a split in the committee over this offer. Half of us want the money; the others dislike the conditions.
- p09-b-17-s02 · Karim: What exactly does the sponsor want?
- p09-b-17-s03 · Nell: A logo on the programme, which seems fine, but also the right to approve every speech.
- p09-b-17-s04 · Karim: Our stance should be clear: sponsorship doesn't buy control over what members say.
- p09-b-17-s05 · Nell: Then should we reject the whole offer?
- p09-b-17-s06 · Karim: Let's first ask them to remove the speech-approval condition. We can keep the logo arrangement on the table.
- p09-b-17-s07 · Nell: Agreed. I'll send that request, but I won't accept the funding on the club's behalf.
- p09-b-17-s08 · Karim: Good. We'll bring their response back to the committee.

The committee’s disagreement is tied to a concrete offer: programme branding plus approval of speeches. Nell and Karim choose to request removal of the speech condition and return the response to the committee, explicitly withholding funding acceptance. The Chinese title and goal correctly describe a request they will make, not sponsor agreement or secured independence.

- p09-b-18-s06 · Lewis: Use the name sheet for their titles and the recording for who said what. Don't attribute the campaign's demand to the council.

## 选定去观景台的平缓湖边路线 · p09-b-20

主题：出行与住宿；情景：选定去观景台的平缓湖边路线（woodland-viewpoint-route-choice）。

沟通目标：比较两条路线的坡度和长度，并选定较长但平缓的湖边路线

背景：游客向步道工作人员询问到湖边观景台的路线。

人物：Greta、Owen。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-20-s01 · Greta: Does the short route to the viewpoint go up that slope?
- p09-b-20-s02 · Owen: Yes. It's quite steep near the top. The lakeside route is longer but mostly level.
- p09-b-20-s03 · Greta: Do the trees surround the whole lake? I'd like to see the surrounding hills, not just the woods.
- p09-b-20-s04 · Owen: There are trees on three sides. The viewpoint faces the open side, so both routes end with the same view.
- p09-b-20-s05 · Greta: How much longer is the lakeside route?
- p09-b-20-s06 · Owen: About twenty minutes. There's one long stretch beside the water before it turns towards the viewpoint.
- p09-b-20-s07 · Greta: Then we'll take that route. We have time, and I'd rather avoid the climb.
- p09-b-20-s08 · Owen: Follow the blue signs from this gate. They'll keep you on the lakeside path.

目标用法：

- slope · n. · 斜坡；义项 sense-staged-ox-4c0fe14e1d5d96cd-p09-b-20；来源 ox-4c0fe14e1d5d96cd；证据 p09-b-20-s01；词形 slope。
- steep · adj. · 陡峭的；义项 sense-staged-ox-8f1cae45ba8cba67-p09-b-20；来源 ox-8f1cae45ba8cba67；证据 p09-b-20-s02；词形 steep。
- surround · v. · 环绕；义项 sense-staged-ox-0b37a92e2934fac2-p09-b-20；来源 ox-0b37a92e2934fac2；证据 p09-b-20-s03；词形 surround。
- surrounding · adj. · 周围的；义项 sense-staged-ox-82033a03e40cb5e2-p09-b-20；来源 ox-82033a03e40cb5e2；证据 p09-b-20-s03；词形 surrounding。
- stretch · n. · 一段路程或区域；义项 sense-staged-ox-b343ad6045f2e5cc-p09-b-20；来源 ox-b343ad6045f2e5cc；证据 p09-b-20-s06；词形 stretch。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Greta checks steepness, tree cover and the extra walking time before choosing the mostly level lakeside route to the same viewpoint. Owen gives blue-sign directions, while no arrival is claimed. The Chinese title and goal correctly name the chosen gentler route and the length-versus-climb trade-off.

绑定文件 SHA-256：8bcb77681679a3d64ca55972514ff53b81185e60c486653bd042194eeef03bc9

- ox-4c0fe14e1d5d96cd：accepted；s01 go up that slope uses the count noun for the inclined ground on the shorter route. 斜坡 fits the selected noun entry rather than the alternative verb.（p09-b-20-s01）。
- ox-8f1cae45ba8cba67：accepted；s02 quite steep near the top describes the route’s gradient and is contrasted with mostly level. The adjective teaches 陡峭的, not a steep price or change.（p09-b-20-s02）。
- ox-0b37a92e2934fac2：accepted；s03 do the trees surround the whole lake uses surround as a transitive verb asking whether trees encircle the water. 环绕 fits, and the reply appropriately limits coverage to three sides.（p09-b-20-s03）。
- ox-82033a03e40cb5e2：accepted；s03 the surrounding hills uses surrounding attributively for hills around the lake. 周围的 is accurate; this is an adjective rather than the progressive verb form.（p09-b-20-s03）。
- ox-b343ad6045f2e5cc：accepted；s06 one long stretch beside the water uses a count noun for a continuous portion of the route before the turn. 一段路程或区域 fits the selected noun sense.（p09-b-20-s06）。

<a id="dialogue-p09-b-21"></a>

## 约定缩小论文的因果结论并补写限制 · p09-b-21

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定缩小论文的因果结论并补写限制（thesis-evidence-limit-revision）。

沟通目标：区分论文描述的细致程度与因果证据的不足，并约定修改结论及补写研究限制

背景：学生与导师讨论虚构小样本课堂项目的论文，不涉及真实研究结论。

人物：Beatrice、Dr Han。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-21-s01 · Beatrice: Could you look at the conclusion of my thesis? I say the new seating plan caused the higher scores.
- p09-b-21-s02 · Dr Han: Your description of the classroom is thorough, but that isn't sufficient evidence of cause and effect.
- p09-b-21-s03 · Beatrice: I checked the scores thoroughly. Every pupil improved.
- p09-b-21-s04 · Dr Han: I can see that. But did you record other changes sufficiently carefully, such as extra practice before the second test?
- p09-b-21-s05 · Beatrice: No. I only recorded the seating and the scores. So I can't rule out extra practice.
- p09-b-21-s06 · Dr Han: Exactly. The scores rose after the seating changed; thus, you can describe an association, not establish the cause.
- p09-b-21-s07 · Beatrice: I'll narrow the conclusion to that association and add the missing information as a limitation.
- p09-b-21-s08 · Dr Han: Good. Keep the careful description. It will help readers understand exactly what you observed.

目标用法：

- thesis · n. · 学位论文；义项 sense-staged-ox-2ead58637428bf76-p09-b-21；来源 ox-2ead58637428bf76；证据 p09-b-21-s01；词形 thesis。
- thorough · adj. · 详尽的；周密的；义项 sense-staged-ox-75f16f4dd1fcf9d8-p09-b-21；来源 ox-75f16f4dd1fcf9d8；证据 p09-b-21-s02；词形 thorough。
- sufficient · adj. · 足够的；义项 sense-staged-ox-1d0e949c19cc4a5a-p09-b-21；来源 ox-1d0e949c19cc4a5a；证据 p09-b-21-s02；词形 sufficient。
- thoroughly · adv. · 仔细而全面地；义项 sense-staged-ox-5c7ba806331d2e6f-p09-b-21；来源 ox-5c7ba806331d2e6f；证据 p09-b-21-s03；词形 thoroughly。
- sufficiently · adv. · 足够地；义项 sense-staged-ox-6890ae12a8398013-p09-b-21；来源 ox-6890ae12a8398013；证据 p09-b-21-s04；词形 sufficiently。
- thus · adv. · 因此；义项 sense-staged-ox-d3891bb7127b7d93-p09-b-21；来源 ox-d3891bb7127b7d93；证据 p09-b-21-s06；词形 thus。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The advisor distinguishes detailed classroom description and checked scores from control of alternative explanations. Beatrice acknowledges unrecorded extra practice and agrees to report association and limitations, not establish causation. The title and goal correctly describe a planned narrowing and limitation section; no completed edit or proven seating effect is asserted.

绑定文件 SHA-256：f99d563f328b26b94daef7818f248f077585af9f293821fa44cfbb7bdb9c7a03

- ox-2ead58637428bf76：accepted；s01 the conclusion of my thesis identifies an academic dissertation under an advisor’s review. The noun fits 学位论文 rather than merely an isolated proposition.（p09-b-21-s01）。
- ox-75f16f4dd1fcf9d8：accepted；s02 description of the classroom is thorough uses a predicate adjective praising detail and care while separating it from causal proof. 详尽的/周密的 is accurate.（p09-b-21-s02）。
- ox-1d0e949c19cc4a5a：accepted；s02 not sufficient evidence of cause and effect uses sufficient attributively with evidence to mean enough for the claim. 足够的 fits the explicitly denied evidential threshold.（p09-b-21-s02）。
- ox-5c7ba806331d2e6f：accepted；s03 checked the scores thoroughly uses an adverb modifying checked for careful comprehensive checking. 仔细而全面地 is appropriate, though that checking alone does not prove causation.（p09-b-21-s03）。
- ox-6890ae12a8398013：accepted；s04 sufficiently carefully is a degree adverb modifying carefully, asking whether recording was careful enough to assess alternatives. 足够地 accurately distinguishes it from the adjective sufficient.（p09-b-21-s04）。
- ox-d3891bb7127b7d93：accepted；s06 thus introduces the evidence-limited conclusion after the observed sequence and the acknowledged missing controls. The connective adverb means 因此; its formal tone fits academic feedback.（p09-b-21-s06）。

## 约定用先写后轮流发言改善青年讨论课 · p09-b-22

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定用先写后轮流发言改善青年讨论课（teen-debate-participation-plan）。

沟通目标：识别试教中少数人垄断发言的问题，并约定先独立写想法再轮流分享

背景：两位青年活动带领者根据一次试教商量讨论课的参与方式。

人物：Zoe、Hamish。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-22-s01 · Zoe: The group are all in their teens, but one teen answered almost every question today.
- p09-b-22-s02 · Hamish: I noticed a tendency for the quieter members to wait until someone else spoke.
- p09-b-22-s03 · Zoe: How can we stimulate discussion without putting them on the spot?
- p09-b-22-s04 · Hamish: Let's tackle that by giving everyone two minutes to write an idea first. Then we can invite one idea from each person.
- p09-b-22-s05 · Zoe: That might strengthen their confidence, especially if passing is allowed.
- p09-b-22-s06 · Hamish: Yes. We'll make it clear that nobody has to speak, and the first volunteer doesn't get every turn.
- p09-b-22-s07 · Zoe: Let's try that next session. I'll prepare the opening question and explain the order.
- p09-b-22-s08 · Hamish: And I'll watch whether more people get a chance, rather than assuming it worked.

目标用法：

- teens · n. · 十三至十九岁的年龄段；义项 sense-staged-ox-5cd1f8a030237f04-p09-b-22；来源 ox-5cd1f8a030237f04；证据 p09-b-22-s01；词形 teens。
- teen · n. · 青少年；义项 sense-staged-ox-0db2a767f711f64c-p09-b-22；来源 ox-0db2a767f711f64c；证据 p09-b-22-s01；词形 teen。
- tendency · n. · 倾向；义项 sense-staged-ox-742afdeb7fe0a6ff-p09-b-22；来源 ox-742afdeb7fe0a6ff；证据 p09-b-22-s02；词形 tendency。
- stimulate · v. · 激发；促进；义项 sense-staged-ox-bdbdd75ccafef404-p09-b-22；来源 ox-bdbdd75ccafef404；证据 p09-b-22-s03；词形 stimulate。
- tackle · v. · 着手解决；义项 sense-staged-ox-61f37c4e68345f01-p09-b-22；来源 ox-61f37c4e68345f01；证据 p09-b-22-s04；词形 tackle。
- strengthen · v. · 增强；义项 sense-staged-ox-33a7d0cb3ac7b654-p09-b-22；来源 ox-33a7d0cb3ac7b654；证据 p09-b-22-s05；词形 strengthen。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The facilitators identify one participant dominating responses and quieter participants waiting. They agree to private writing followed by invitations in turn, explicitly allow passing and will observe participation rather than assume success. The Chinese title and goal describe the agreed method and do not claim it has already improved the next session; British collective agreement in the group are is natural.

绑定文件 SHA-256：fbe6b92ae0f81154e4fcc30260df8eb49daa61e79ae70c4c182128a60ea799c8

- ox-5cd1f8a030237f04：accepted；s01 in their teens is the conventional plural noun phrase for the thirteen-to-nineteen age range. The Chinese gloss correctly distinguishes this age period from the separate noun teen for a person.（p09-b-22-s01）。
- ox-0db2a767f711f64c：accepted；s01 one teen answered almost every question uses teen as the singular count noun for one adolescent. 青少年 fits, and the source’s adjective alternative is not selected.（p09-b-22-s01）。
- ox-742afdeb7fe0a6ff：accepted；s02 a tendency for the quieter members to wait is the noun construction for an observed behavioral inclination. 倾向 matches and does not turn the observation into an inevitable rule.（p09-b-22-s02）。
- ox-bdbdd75ccafef404：accepted；s03 stimulate discussion is a natural transitive collocation for encouraging discussion. 激发/促进 fits the facilitator’s question without implying force or physical stimulation.（p09-b-22-s03）。
- ox-61f37c4e68345f01：accepted；s04 tackle that by giving everyone two minutes uses the verb for addressing the participation problem with a specific method. 着手解决 fits, not the noun for sports equipment or a physical tackle.（p09-b-22-s04）。
- ox-33a7d0cb3ac7b654：accepted；s05 might strengthen their confidence uses a transitive verb for making confidence stronger. 增强 matches, with might correctly leaving the effect uncertain.（p09-b-22-s05）。

## 选定罢工期间可直达机场的巴士 · p09-b-23

主题：出行与住宿；情景：选定罢工期间可直达机场的巴士（strike-airport-transfer-check）。

沟通目标：核对机场巴士的当日停靠点和行李限制，并选定不在郊区停靠的直达班次

背景：虚构交通咨询：旅客在铁路罢工日询问巴士，所有班次信息只属于对话情境。

人物：Nora、Ticket Clerk Sam。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-23-s01 · Nora: With the rail strike today, can I take your nine o'clock bus to the airport?
- p09-b-23-s02 · Ticket Clerk Sam: Yes. That service is running and goes directly to terminal two.
- p09-b-23-s03 · Nora: Does it stop in the suburb of Belton? My sister lives there.
- p09-b-23-s04 · Ticket Clerk Sam: No. It's strictly an airport service today. The town bus still serves Belton, but it doesn't go to the airport.
- p09-b-23-s05 · Nora: Then I'll visit her another day. Is one suitcase and a small backpack all right?
- p09-b-23-s06 · Ticket Clerk Sam: Yes. We have a strict one-suitcase limit per passenger, plus one small bag you can keep with you.
- p09-b-23-s07 · Nora: That works. I'll take the nine o'clock airport bus.
- p09-b-23-s08 · Ticket Clerk Sam: Board at bay six. Tickets are sold at the machine beside the bay.

目标用法：

- strike · n. · 罢工；义项 sense-staged-ox-0a8784f32873ab39-p09-b-23；来源 ox-0a8784f32873ab39；证据 p09-b-23-s01；词形 strike。
- terminal · n. · 航站楼；义项 sense-staged-ox-fd6e091a590d6ece-p09-b-23；来源 ox-fd6e091a590d6ece；证据 p09-b-23-s02；词形 terminal。
- suburb · n. · 郊区；义项 sense-staged-ox-bac7c75d9707a46d-p09-b-23；来源 ox-bac7c75d9707a46d；证据 p09-b-23-s03；词形 suburb。
- strictly · adv. · 仅限于；完全是；义项 sense-staged-ox-e38b8d8ef75a500a-p09-b-23；来源 ox-e38b8d8ef75a500a；证据 p09-b-23-s04；词形 strictly。
- strict · adj. · 严格的；义项 sense-staged-ox-4a2c7cab7d10891d-p09-b-23；来源 ox-4a2c7cab7d10891d；证据 p09-b-23-s06；词形 strict。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The traveller verifies a running nine-o’clock airport bus, learns it will not serve her sister’s suburb and checks the precise two-piece luggage allowance. She selects that service and receives boarding and purchase directions. The Chinese title and goal accurately describe the route choice rather than completed ticket payment or boarding.

绑定文件 SHA-256：691ee49911b8120a4ae1f04630ab73c91e8eedaa65501a01bec62dc7b2bbdc43

- ox-0a8784f32873ab39：accepted；s01 the rail strike names industrial action affecting railway travel. The noun means 罢工 and explains the bus alternative, not a blow or verb action.（p09-b-23-s01）。
- ox-fd6e091a590d6ece：accepted；s02 terminal two identifies the airport building reached by the bus. The selected noun matches 航站楼, not the C1 adjective alternative.（p09-b-23-s02）。
- ox-bac7c75d9707a46d：accepted；s03 the suburb of Belton uses a count noun for the outlying residential district where Nora’s sister lives. 郊区 fits the stop enquiry.（p09-b-23-s03）。
- ox-e38b8d8ef75a500a：accepted；s04 strictly an airport service restricts the bus’s purpose and destinations on that day. The adverb means 仅限于/完全是, not a manner of enforcing a rule.（p09-b-23-s04）。
- ox-4a2c7cab7d10891d：accepted；s06 a strict one-suitcase limit uses the adjective to mark an enforced luggage constraint while separately allowing a small bag. 严格的 correctly modifies limit.（p09-b-23-s06）。

## 选定柔和色调的肖像画作为礼物 · p09-b-24

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选定柔和色调的肖像画作为礼物（portrait-colour-stroke-choice）。

沟通目标：比较两幅肖像的色调和笔触，并选定柔和版本送给母亲

背景：画廊访客与朋友比较同一人物的两幅肖像版画。

人物：Sofia、Cal。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-24-s01 · Sofia: The red version is striking. You notice it as soon as you enter the room.
- p09-b-24-s02 · Cal: It is, but your mother's room is quite restful. Look at the softer tone of this blue one.
- p09-b-24-s03 · Sofia: I love the way one stroke of the brush suggests the whole shoulder. Is that detail in both versions?
- p09-b-24-s04 · Cal: Yes. They're prints from the same drawing, with different colours added.
- p09-b-24-s05 · Sofia: The red one is stunning on this wall, but I think Mum would live more happily with the blue one.
- p09-b-24-s06 · Cal: Then choose the blue. A present doesn't have to be the boldest thing in the gallery.
- p09-b-24-s07 · Sofia: You're right. That's the one I'll ask the assistant for.

目标用法：

- striking · adj. · 引人注目的；义项 sense-staged-ox-d6d44145bca1cb01-p09-b-24；来源 ox-d6d44145bca1cb01；证据 p09-b-24-s01；词形 striking。
- tone · n. · 色调；义项 sense-staged-ox-0614660ad213de7d-p09-b-24；来源 ox-0614660ad213de7d；证据 p09-b-24-s02；词形 tone。
- stroke · n. · 一笔；笔画；义项 sense-staged-ox-785f001ca9cc2243-p09-b-24；来源 ox-785f001ca9cc2243；证据 p09-b-24-s03；词形 stroke。
- stunning · adj. · 极美的；令人惊艳的；义项 sense-staged-ox-064984474e8681fe-p09-b-24；来源 ox-064984474e8681fe；证据 p09-b-24-s05；词形 stunning。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Sofia and Cal compare coloured print versions of the same portrait, notice shared brushwork and weigh gallery impact against her mother’s restful room. Sofia selects the blue one and will ask the assistant for it; the Chinese title and goal claim selection for a gift, not completed payment or delivery. The shared shoulder detail supports the portrait context.

绑定文件 SHA-256：b56ea63ba666819f3f47de155d4190519df2004f7250fdb83ad58117e8fed03f

- ox-d6d44145bca1cb01：accepted；s01 the red version is striking is immediately explained by noticing it on entering. The adjective means 引人注目的, not an ongoing action of striking.（p09-b-24-s01）。
- ox-0614660ad213de7d：accepted；s02 the softer tone of this blue one refers to its visual colour quality, contrasted with the bold red version. The noun is 色调 rather than voice or attitude.（p09-b-24-s02）。
- ox-785f001ca9cc2243：accepted；s03 one stroke of the brush suggests the whole shoulder uses the count noun for a brush mark in the picture. 一笔/笔画 accurately names the artistic mark, not a medical event.（p09-b-24-s03）。
- ox-064984474e8681fe：accepted；s05 the red one is stunning on this wall evaluates its impressive beauty while deciding another version better suits the recipient. 极美的/令人惊艳的 fits the adjective without implying a physical stunning action.（p09-b-24-s05）。

## 请康复服务安排治疗师回电 · p09-b-25

主题：健康与照护；情景：请康复服务安排治疗师回电（post-surgery-therapy-role-inquiry）。

沟通目标：说明术后日常活动困难，澄清希望先与治疗师讨论评估，并请求服务方安排回电

背景：虚构术后服务沟通练习：来电者只询问评估和回电流程，对话不提供诊断或治疗方案。

人物：Ruth、Receptionist Dan。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-25-s01 · Ruth: I had surgery last month, and I still struggle with ordinary things like getting dressed. I'm not sure whom to contact.
- p09-b-25-s02 · Receptionist Dan: Have you already spoken to the surgeon about the difficulties?
- p09-b-25-s03 · Ruth: Yes. She suggested I contact your service about therapy, but I don't know what the first visit involves.
- p09-b-25-s04 · Receptionist Dan: The therapist can explain the assessment and discuss your needs. I can ask her to call you before you book anything.
- p09-b-25-s05 · Ruth: Please do. I'd rather talk it through first than choose an appointment without understanding it.
- p09-b-25-s06 · Receptionist Dan: Certainly. Is the number on your referral still the best one?
- p09-b-25-s07 · Ruth: Yes, and I'm available after two this afternoon.
- p09-b-25-s08 · Receptionist Dan: I'll pass on that number and your availability. The therapist will need to confirm when she can call.

目标用法：

- surgery · n. · 外科手术；义项 sense-staged-ox-bc857bf560841ad6-p09-b-25；来源 ox-bc857bf560841ad6；证据 p09-b-25-s01；词形 surgery。
- struggle · v. · 费力地做；艰难应对；义项 sense-staged-ox-c34f366a9dad631a-p09-b-25；来源 ox-c34f366a9dad631a；证据 p09-b-25-s01；词形 struggle。
- surgeon · n. · 外科医生；义项 sense-staged-ox-030f7d1ceac2a6ef-p09-b-25；来源 ox-030f7d1ceac2a6ef；证据 p09-b-25-s02；词形 surgeon。

沟通目标：核对虚构影片的幸存者视角与悲剧内容，并决定改用轻松短片安排家庭放映

- p09-b-26-s03 · Esther: Does it actually show the accident?
- p09-b-26-s04 · Malik: Briefly, yes. Much of the film is about his terror during the storm and his grief afterwards.
- p09-b-26-s05 · Esther: That could terrify the younger children. I don't think this is the right evening for a tragic story.
- p09-b-26-s06 · Malik: Agreed. We could use the light-hearted short film we watched last week instead.
- p09-b-26-s07 · Esther: Let's do that. We know it suits the group, and nobody has to sit through the shipwreck film.

The Chinese title and goal correctly report rejecting the shipwreck film for this family screening and choosing an alternative, not a screening already held.

## 决定先联系捐赠者再处理外套与照片 · p09-b-27

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：决定先联系捐赠者再处理外套与照片（donated-coat-label-handling）。

- p09-b-27-s02 · Imran: Not yet. Read the tag: 'Please contact me before recycling.' There's a phone number underneath.
- p09-b-27-s03 · Faye: And there's a photograph tucked into the pocket. It's caught on the lining.
- p09-b-27-s04 · Imran: Don't tear it while you're pulling it out. Let's leave it there for now.
- p09-b-27-s05 · Faye: I can read the note on the back: 'Dad's last picture'. This may be a real treasure to the person who donated the coat.
- p09-b-27-s06 · Imran: Then we should ask them about both the photograph and the coat before doing anything else.
- p09-b-27-s07 · Faye: Agreed. I'll put the whole coat in the hold box with the tag still attached, and call the donor.
- p09-b-27-s08 · Imran: Thanks. We won't send it for recycling until we've spoken to them.

The volunteers first consider recycling a damaged coat, then notice the attached contact request and an apparently sentimental photograph caught in its pocket. They agree to keep the coat and tag together and contact the donor before disposal. The Chinese title and goal correctly state the decision and objects; no donor reply, retrieval or recycling is invented.

## 约定删除虚构报道中未经证实的身份标签 · p09-b-28

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定删除虚构报道中未经证实的身份标签（fiction-news-terrorism-label-check）。

沟通目标：区分图片说明中的军队位置与袭击归责，并约定删除没有证据支持的恐怖主义标签

背景：课堂使用完全虚构的地图、图片与报道片段练习核对证据，不涉及现实政治事件。

人物：Mina、Tutor Ellis。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-28-s01 · Mina: I've labelled the rider in this picture a terrorist. The exercise says there was an attack nearby.
- p09-b-28-s02 · Tutor Ellis: Does any of the material identify that rider as taking part in the attack?
- p09-b-28-s03 · Mina: No. The caption only identifies a cavalry troop in the northern territory.
- p09-b-28-s04 · Tutor Ellis: Then a location isn't evidence of responsibility. And the background note says the investigators haven't established a motive.
- p09-b-28-s05 · Mina: So I can't describe the incident as terrorism on the basis of this picture.
- p09-b-28-s06 · Tutor Ellis: Correct. Report what the exercise actually gives you, and keep the unknowns visible.
- p09-b-28-s07 · Mina: I'll remove that label from the rider and the claim about the incident. I'll keep the caption's location and say responsibility hasn't been established.

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

In an explicitly fictional evidence exercise, the tutor separates a cavalry unit’s pictured location from participation in an attack and an unestablished motive. Mina commits to removing both the rider’s label and the incident characterization while retaining supported location and uncertainty. The Chinese title and goal accurately describe a planned removal, not a proven identity or completed edit.
