- ox-aef6a3b201de1b5e：accepted；s03 a cavalry troop uses troop as a singular count noun for a unit of mounted soldiers. The selected sense 一队骑兵 is supported explicitly by cavalry, not confused with an individual soldier.（p09-b-28-s03）。

## 纠正课堂贸易表格的重量和金额单位 · p09-b-29

主题：学习与技能；情景：纠正课堂贸易表格的重量和金额单位（market-data-unit-reconciliation）。

沟通目标：区分货物重量与交易金额，并纠正把一万亿误读为十亿的错误

- p09-b-29-s03 · Ronan: Right, a thousand billion. I missed the unit above the column.
- p09-b-29-s04 · Suki: You've also copied a weight into the money column. This row says one tonne of grain; it doesn't give the price.
- p09-b-29-s05 · Ronan: So I should keep the weight separate and mark the value as not provided.
- p09-b-29-s06 · Suki: Exactly. We can't turn a quantity of grain into a price without more information.
- p09-b-29-s07 · Ronan: I've corrected both cells now: one trillion credits for the total, and one tonne with no value listed for that shipment.
- p09-b-29-s08 · Suki: Those match the table. Let's keep the units in the headings when we copy it.

- trading · n. · 贸易；交易活动；义项 sense-staged-ox-b39e7f6e82b637db-p09-b-29；来源 ox-b39e7f6e82b637db；证据 p09-b-29-s01；词形 trading。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The pair distinguish value measured in trillions from weight measured in tonnes and refuse to infer a missing shipment price. Unlike the prospective revisions elsewhere, s07 explicitly says both cells have been corrected and s08 verifies they match the table. Therefore the Chinese title and goal’s correction outcome is actually achieved, including the one-trillion versus one-billion distinction.

## 约定为角色转变补足动机并调整场景次序 · p09-b-30

主题：文娱与运动；情景：约定为角色转变补足动机并调整场景次序（character-transition-motive-edit）。

沟通目标：指出角色转变过快的问题，并约定将警告放在冒险决定前、补充试探性行动

- p09-b-30-s01 · Verity: Mara's main trait is caution, but in the next scene she takes a huge risk. Does the transition feel convincing?
- p09-b-30-s02 · Omar: Not yet. One friendly conversation can't transform her entire outlook. What changes her mind?
- p09-b-30-s03 · Verity: She subsequently learns that the bridge is closing. In the current draft, that news comes after she's already decided to leave.
- p09-b-30-s04 · Omar: Move the warning earlier. Then her decision has a reason, rather than just serving the plot.
- p09-b-30-s05 · Verity: And in the subsequent scene, she could test the first part of the route before committing to the whole journey.
- p09-b-30-s06 · Omar: Yes. That preserves her caution while showing why she's willing to act.
- p09-b-30-s07 · Verity: I'll move the warning before the decision and add that small test afterwards.
- p09-b-30-s08 · Omar: Good. Then we can read the three scenes together and see whether the change feels earned.

- trait · n. · 性格特点；义项 sense-staged-ox-62a18f2c77d10d3a-p09-b-30；来源 ox-62a18f2c77d10d3a；证据 p09-b-30-s01；词形 trait。

- subsequent · adj. · 随后的；义项 sense-staged-ox-2dc0d8dbf946f1fe-p09-b-30；来源 ox-2dc0d8dbf946f1fe；证据 p09-b-30-s05；词形 subsequent。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The writers diagnose an abrupt move from caution to risk and notice that motivating bridge-closure news currently arrives after the decision. They agree to move the warning earlier and insert a small route test before full commitment, then reread. The Chinese title and goal accurately describe this revision plan, not an already transformed character or completed rewrite.

## 约定先核实篮子纹样的代表范围 · p09-b-31

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定先核实篮子纹样的代表范围（museum-tribal-attribution-check）。

沟通目标：核对虚构目录中的制作者归属，并约定在确认前不把一个篮子的纹样推广到整个部族

背景：博物馆课程使用虚构部族与物品目录训练标签写作，避免把个体作品当作整个群体的统一特征。

人物：Carmen、Dr Bell。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-31-s01 · Carmen: The catalogue says this basket was made by a member of the Aruni tribe. Can I call the pattern a typical Aruni design?
- p09-b-31-s02 · Dr Bell: Not from that information alone. Does the catalogue say whether other makers used the same pattern?
- p09-b-31-s03 · Carmen: No. It gives the maker's name, says they're Aruni and records where the basket was collected. It doesn't mention patterns used by other makers.
- p09-b-31-s04 · Dr Bell: Then keep those details. We should ask the curator what evidence there is for a wider claim.
- p09-b-31-s05 · Carmen: I'll do that. Until we have an answer, the label will describe this basket, not the whole tribe.
- p09-b-31-s06 · Dr Bell: Exactly. A precise label can still be interesting without claiming more than we know.

目标用法：

- tribe · n. · 部族；部落；义项 sense-staged-ox-5c4b493bf78f65b7-p09-b-31；来源 ox-5c4b493bf78f65b7；证据 p09-b-31-s01、p09-b-31-s05；词形 tribe。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Freshly reread all six turns, the complete metadata and the selected target in content version 2. s01 reports the maker’s Aruni affiliation, and repaired s03 now consistently retains that affiliation, the maker’s name and the collection location while explicitly distinguishing the missing information about other makers’ patterns. Dr Bell’s question and advice therefore support a precise evidence-limitation lesson rather than contradicting the catalogue. s04 proposes asking the curator; s05 agrees to do so and to limit the label to this basket until an answer exists. The Chinese title and goal correctly describe checking the known affiliation and an agreement to seek broader evidence, not an obtained curator confirmation, completed relabelling or established typical tribal design. The fictional museum-course context, speaker roles and closing endorsement remain coherent.

- ox-5c4b493bf78f65b7：accepted；s01 a member of the Aruni tribe uses tribe as a singular count noun naming the fictional social group; repaired s03 confirms that affiliation is actually recorded. In s05 the whole tribe is the same noun with a collective scope, explicitly contrasted with one basket to avoid unsupported generalization. Both mapped forms are tribe, the selected B2 noun source ox-5c4b493bf78f65b7 fits, and 部族/部落 accurately expresses the intended sense.（p09-b-31-s01、p09-b-31-s05）。

## 确认代喂猫的份量与关门检查 · p09-b-32

主题：自然与环境；情景：确认代喂猫的份量与关门检查（pet-care-feeding-and-door-handover）。

沟通目标：核对主人准备的喂食安排，并约定不给额外零食、关门前确认猫的位置

背景：虚构宠物照看交接：照看者按主人留下的既有安排执行，不提供兽医建议。

人物：Arthur、Lucie。

起草：author-phase09-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-b-32-s01 · Arthur: The two labelled tubs are for tomorrow: one in the morning and one in the evening.
- p09-b-32-s02 · Lucie: He keeps asking for food. Are you sure he won't starve between meals?
- p09-b-32-s03 · Arthur: He does that even when he's just eaten. Please use the portions I've left and don't spoil him with extra treats.
- p09-b-32-s04 · Lucie: Understood. Is there anything else I should check before I leave?
- p09-b-32-s05 · Arthur: He likes hiding in the cupboard. Make sure you don't trap him inside when you shut the door.
- p09-b-32-s06 · Lucie: I'll check where he is before closing it, and stick to the two tubs. What if I'm unsure about anything?
- p09-b-32-s07 · Arthur: Call me rather than guessing. I'll have my phone with me.
- p09-b-32-s08 · Lucie: All right. I'll come at eight tomorrow morning for the first visit.

目标用法：

- starve · v. · 挨饿；因缺乏食物而受苦；义项 sense-staged-ox-053a583633ed6e04-p09-b-32；来源 ox-053a583633ed6e04；证据 p09-b-32-s02；词形 starve。
- spoil · v. · 宠坏；溺爱；义项 sense-staged-ox-df46652a84201cf3-p09-b-32；来源 ox-df46652a84201cf3；证据 p09-b-32-s03；词形 spoil。
- trap · v. · 使困住；义项 sense-staged-ox-42b781a4dad0e700-p09-b-32；来源 ox-42b781a4dad0e700；证据 p09-b-32-s05；词形 trap。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The owner supplies two pre-portioned tubs, rejects extra treats, and instructs the sitter to check the pet’s location before shutting the cupboard. Lucie agrees and fixes the first visit for eight tomorrow, with the owner available for questions. The title establishes the cat-care setting for the pronoun he, and the goal correctly describes confirming instructions and agreeing future checks rather than feeding or checking already completed.

- ox-053a583633ed6e04：accepted；s02 will not starve between meals uses the intransitive verb in an anxious, colloquially exaggerated question about going without food. 挨饿/因缺乏食物而受苦 fits; the next turn explains the begging behavior without asserting actual starvation.（p09-b-32-s02）。
- ox-df46652a84201cf3：accepted；s03 do not spoil him with extra treats uses the transitive verb for overindulging the pet. 宠坏/溺爱 is the selected sense, not causing the food itself to decay.（p09-b-32-s03）。

## 限定档案试办并安排复核 · p09-c-05

主题：工作与协作；情景：限定档案试办范围和复核承诺（archive-pilot-goal-and-review）。

- p09-c-05-s03 · Omar: Then let's start with one box. We can test whether people can find five named letters.
- p09-c-05-s04 · Tess: That would tell us more than simply counting scanned pages.
- p09-c-05-s05 · Omar: Ultimately, the search results should decide whether we expand, not the launch date.
- p09-c-05-s06 · Tess: Agreed. I'll choose the box today and bring the search results to Friday's meeting.
- p09-c-05-s07 · Omar: I'll write up that limited plan. We aren't promising the full archive yet.

- p09-c-06-s03 · Anika: He appears unconscious. He hasn't answered when I've spoken to him.

- p09-c-06-s05 · Anika: No. I'm not a witness to what happened. I arrived a moment ago, and I can see a wound on his wrist.

## 核对工厂岗位的工资与人数信息 · p09-c-12

主题：工作与协作；情景：核对招聘工资和员工人数图表（factory-weekly-pay-inquiry）。

沟通目标：区分基本周薪和加班报酬，澄清员工人数图不是工资增长图并决定索取岗位说明

背景：虚构工厂招聘咨询，求职者询问装配岗位的信息，不构成实际工作要约。

人物：Finn、Rosa。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-12-s01 · Finn: For the assembly job at your factory, is the weekly wage on this poster the basic pay, or does it include overtime?
- p09-c-12-s02 · Rosa: It's the basic pay for the stated hours. Overtime is listed separately in the job description.
- p09-c-12-s03 · Finn: The line on this chart goes upward quite sharply. Does that show pay increasing?
- p09-c-12-s04 · Rosa: No. It shows the size of our workforce over five years, not anyone's pay.
- p09-c-12-s05 · Finn: Thanks for explaining. Could I read about training and safety in the workplace before I apply?
- p09-c-12-s06 · Rosa: Of course. I'll send you the full job description, including the hours and pay details.
- p09-c-12-s07 · Finn: Please do. I'll read that before deciding whether to apply.

## 商定首次志愿接待的分工 · p09-c-13

- p09-c-13-s01 · Mae: I'm willing to help at the welcome session, but I can't promise every Saturday.
- p09-c-13-s02 · Idris: That's fine. It's voluntary, and we arrange each session separately.
- p09-c-13-s03 · Mae: Could I work with someone the first time? I don't know the building yet.
- p09-c-13-s04 · Idris: Yes. Whoever takes the reception desk will show you the sign-in sheet. This Saturday, that will be me.
- p09-c-13-s05 · Mae: Good. To whom should I send a message if my plans change?
- p09-c-13-s06 · Idris: Send it directly to me. For now, shall we put you down to help me with sign-in at ten?
- p09-c-13-s07 · Mae: Yes, that suits me. I'll join you at ten.

## 缩小小麦气候项目的比较范围 · p09-c-14

主题：研究与论证；情景：限定小麦气候研究的资料比较范围（crop-project-source-comparison）。

沟通目标：区分长期变暖资料和单年产量，约定比较同一地区的多年记录并保留其他影响因素

背景：两名学生讨论课堂农业研究选题，不报告现实气候新闻或提供种植建议。

人物：Eli、Zara。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-14-s01 · Eli: I'd like our agricultural project to ask how warming might affect wheat production.
- p09-c-14-s02 · Zara: Then we need more than last year's harvest figure. One poor harvest doesn't establish a long-term pattern.
- p09-c-14-s03 · Eli: Could we compare the class dataset's temperature records with its harvest records for the same region?
- p09-c-14-s04 · Zara: Yes, over the full twenty years. We should also note the years with floods or changes in irrigation.
- p09-c-14-s05 · Eli: So we shouldn't describe every decline as an adverse effect of higher temperatures.
- p09-c-14-s06 · Zara: Exactly. Let's build the comparison first and list those other possible influences beside it.

- p09-c-15-s01 · Cora: My draft says there's universal support for removing the parking spaces.
- p09-c-15-s02 · Ben: We spoke to eight people at a cycling event. That's one viewpoint, not the whole neighbourhood.
- p09-c-15-s03 · Cora: Could I say support is widespread instead?
- p09-c-15-s04 · Ben: We don't know that either. The idea may be widely discussed, but discussion isn't agreement.
- p09-c-15-s05 · Cora: Then I'll write that all eight people we interviewed supported it, and explain where we found them.
- p09-c-15-s06 · Ben: Yes. Add that we haven't yet heard from drivers or nearby shop owners.
- p09-c-15-s07 · Cora: Agreed. I'll remove the broad claim and keep those limits visible.

## 核对肖像画说明中的买主财富与购藏记录 · p09-c-16

主题：文娱与运动；情景：根据购藏记录核对富商所购肖像画的说明（museum-portrait-acquisition-label）。

人物：Lin、George。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-16-s01 · Lin: The label says this wealthy merchant bought the portrait to impress his rivals. Do we have a source for that?
- p09-c-16-s02 · George: No. The archive records his wealth and the date of the acquisition, but not his motive.
- p09-c-16-s03 · Lin: Then we should remove that sentence. The purchase is documented, whereas the rivalry is only a story from a later guidebook.
- p09-c-16-s04 · George: Could we say he bought it for its aesthetic value?
- p09-c-16-s05 · Lin: That's another guess about what he thought. We can describe the colours without claiming to know why he bought it.
- p09-c-16-s06 · George: You're right. I'll keep the acquisition date, cite the archive card, and cut the sentence about his motive.

## 先了解狗的收养评估并约好见面 · p09-c-17

主题：自然与环境；情景：了解收养评估并选择先见面而不带走狗（shelter-adoption-first-visit-boundary）。

沟通目标：说明对狗的行为及福利的担忧，确认需要评估并约定先在工作人员陪同下见面

背景：虚构动物收容所咨询，访客询问照片中的狗 Moss，不在本次对话中办理收养。

人物：Sana、Kit。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-17-s01 · Sana: Is Moss, the dog in your window photo, available for adoption? I felt real affection for him when I saw it.
- p09-c-17-s02 · Kit: He's looking for a home, but we arrange a meeting and an assessment before any decision.
- p09-c-17-s03 · Sana: His description mentions aggression around food. I don't have experience with that.
- p09-c-17-s04 · Kit: Thank you for telling me. His welfare and a suitable match matter more than a quick placement.
- p09-c-17-s05 · Sana: Could I meet him with a member of staff rather than take him out on my own?
- p09-c-17-s06 · Kit: Yes. We can arrange a supervised meeting here on Saturday at eleven, with no commitment to adopt.
- p09-c-17-s07 · Sana: Let's do that. I'll come to learn more, not to take him home that day.

## 肯定打完全场的进步并选择传球练习 · p09-c-20

主题：文娱与运动；情景：承认进步并针对薄弱点选择下一次训练（team-season-progress-next-practice）。

人物：Ada、Marcus。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-20-s01 · Ada: We didn't get a victory, so it feels as if the whole month was wasted.
- p09-c-20-s02 · Marcus: You played the full match for the first time. That's an accomplishment, even though we lost.
- p09-c-20-s03 · Ada: My passing is still a weakness. I gave the ball away three times.
- p09-c-20-s04 · Marcus: Then it would be wise to practise short passes before we add more shooting drills.
- p09-c-20-s05 · Ada: My old coach's favourite piece of wisdom was, 'Work on what the match shows you.' I suppose that's what you're saying.
- p09-c-20-s06 · Marcus: Exactly. Shall we spend the first ten minutes on passing next time?
- p09-c-20-s07 · Ada: Yes. I can be pleased that I finished and still work on that.

## 核对旅行练习日期并商定先核实再订票的答案 · p09-c-22

- p09-c-22-s03 · Arun: That's an unexpected problem. I read the end date and missed the start date.
- p09-c-22-s04 · Faye: Then we shouldn't treat the document as covering the proposed departure.
- p09-c-22-s05 · Arun: For my answer, I'll say the traveller should ask the issuing office to clarify the dates before booking.
- p09-c-22-s06 · Faye: Yes. Don't invent an exception or assume the date can be changed.
- p09-c-22-s07 · Arun: Agreed. In our answer, the traveller's next step is to ask, not to buy a ticket.

## 请求临床人员解答诊疗前用药问题 · p09-c-23

主题：健康与照护；情景：请求临床人员回电解答诊疗前用药疑问（fictional-preprocedure-medication-message）。

沟通目标：说明计划接受诊疗及用药疑问，请接待员安排临床人员当日回电

背景：虚构诊所咨询语言练习，仅请求转交疑问，不给出用药或诊疗建议。

人物：Nadia、Receptionist。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-23-s01 · Nadia: I'm due to undergo a procedure on Friday, and I have a question about a vitamin supplement I take.
- p09-c-23-s02 · Receptionist: I can ask a clinician to call you. Do you need an assessment for a new problem today?
- p09-c-23-s03 · Nadia: No. I don't have acute pain or a new symptom. I just don't understand the preparation letter.
- p09-c-23-s04 · Receptionist: What part would you like the clinician to explain?
- p09-c-23-s05 · Nadia: It says a nurse will administer the medicine here. I'm unsure what I'm supposed to bring from home.
- p09-c-23-s06 · Receptionist: I'll put that question in the message. It's vital that the clinical team, rather than reception, answers the medication question.
- p09-c-23-s07 · Nadia: Could they call today? I'll be available after two.
- p09-c-23-s08 · Receptionist: I'll request a call after two today. I can't confirm a time until the team responds.

## 请求私下了解职场投诉流程 · p09-c-24

主题：工作与协作；情景：约定私下了解职场投诉流程并先核对笔记（workplace-abuse-private-report）。

沟通目标：说明不愿在公开会议中处理辱骂问题，请代表在私下会面时解释流程并先核对记录

背景：虚构工作场所支持咨询，不涉及真实个人指控。

人物：Leah、Union representative。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-24-s01 · Leah: The verbal abuse in yesterday's meeting was unacceptable. I want to report it, but not in another meeting with everyone there.
- p09-c-24-s02 · Union representative: We can talk privately first. You don't have to give me the full account in this reception area.
- p09-c-24-s03 · Leah: My colleague keeps saying I should send a message to the whole office.
- p09-c-24-s04 · Union representative: I would urge you to decide what you're comfortable sharing after we discuss the reporting process.
- p09-c-24-s05 · Leah: Could we meet in the side room now? I'd like you to explain the process and read the notes I've brought before anything is sent.
- p09-c-24-s06 · Union representative: Yes. We'll talk there, and I won't pass on your notes without checking with you.
- p09-c-24-s07 · Leah: Thank you. That's the first step I want.

目标用法：

- abuse · n. · 辱骂；恶言伤害；义项 sense-staged-ox-618604c0c3e70c4c-p09-c-24；来源 ox-618604c0c3e70c4c；证据 p09-c-24-s01；词形 abuse。
- unacceptable · adj. · 不能接受的；义项 sense-staged-ox-fecd3d62a15cd479-p09-c-24；来源 ox-fecd3d62a15cd479；证据 p09-c-24-s01；词形 unacceptable。
- urge · v. · 敦促；力劝；义项 sense-staged-ox-e043ee3cd5c861ec-p09-c-24；来源 ox-e043ee3cd5c861ec；证据 p09-c-24-s04；词形 urge。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-c；整篇结论：accepted。

完整复读第二版：新标签只称约定私下了解流程并先核对笔记，与s05请求旁室会面、解释流程和阅读笔记，s06同意私谈且转交前另核对的承诺一致。标题及目标也保留请求和将来会面状态，已去除流程已明确和请求转交的错误；结尾仅确认第一步，不假称完成正式投诉、公开发信或授予转发权限。

## 商定俱乐部取消费用提案的表决程序 · p09-c-25

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：安排废除俱乐部费用提案的投票程序（club-fee-abolition-vote-proposal）。

沟通目标：区分邀请嘉宾发言和会员表决的权限，约定先介绍提案再投票

背景：虚构运动俱乐部讨论会员会议流程，不涉及现实政治活动。

人物：Jules、Neri。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-25-s01 · Jules: I'd like the club to abolish the extra fee for using the adapted equipment. Can we put that proposal on the agenda?
- p09-c-25-s02 · Neri: Yes. The activist we invited could explain the barriers she's heard about from disabled athletes.
- p09-c-25-s03 · Jules: I advocate removing the fee, but I don't want her to feel responsible for persuading every member.
- p09-c-25-s04 · Neri: Agreed. She's our guest, not a voting member. We can invite her to speak without asking her to defend our budget.
- p09-c-25-s05 · Jules: Then let's introduce the costs ourselves, hear her contribution, and allow questions before voting begins.
- p09-c-25-s06 · Neri: That order works. I'll add the proposal and the voting procedure to the agenda for members to read beforehand.

## 核对出行补助用途并明确记录责任 · p09-c-26

主题：工作与协作；情景：核对补助用途及责任并分配记录工作（grant-travel-allocation-record）。

沟通目标：按给定资助条件保留出行预算，明确单次补助上限、记录分工和最终负责者

背景：虚构项目预算交接练习，金额仅为情景材料。

人物：Imani、Oscar。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-26-s01 · Imani: The grant gives us a travel allocation of two hundred pounds. Can we allocate any of it to refreshments?
- p09-c-26-s02 · Oscar: Not in accordance with this agreement. That money is reserved for participants' journeys.
- p09-c-26-s03 · Imani: Understood. The travel allowance is ten pounds per person for each session, with receipts, isn't it?
- p09-c-26-s04 · Oscar: Yes. Those are the limits in the letter. If fewer people claim, we record the unused balance rather than spend it elsewhere.
- p09-c-26-s05 · Imani: I'll adjust the worksheet accordingly and keep the refreshments on a separate line.
- p09-c-26-s06 · Oscar: For accountability, each payment needs a receipt and the name of the person who checked it.
- p09-c-26-s07 · Imani: I'll check the receipts. Who is accountable for the final report?
- p09-c-26-s08 · Oscar: I am. You keep the payment records, and I'll reconcile the total before submitting the report.
- p09-c-26-s09 · Imani: Agreed. I'll bring you the records after each session.

- allocation · n. · 分配的款项；义项 sense-staged-ox-3547fad676324594-p09-c-26；来源 ox-3547fad676324594；证据 p09-c-26-s01；词形 allocation。

## 纠正虚构案件报道中的事实断言 · p09-c-27

主题：学习与技能；情景：修正虚构案件报道中的指控和证实状态（fictional-court-report-evidence-words）。

沟通目标：区分指控和已证实事实，依据课堂材料选择保留归属及否认信息的报道句

背景：新闻写作课讨论完全虚构的案件材料，不代表现实指控或法律意见。

人物：Esme、Victor。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-27-s01 · Esme: My opening says the accused took the missing camera. Is that supported by our exercise file?
- p09-c-27-s02 · Victor: No. That's the accusation, not a finding. The file says the hearing hasn't happened yet.
- p09-c-27-s03 · Esme: Two witnesses allege that they saw him leave with a black bag. Could that support the sentence?
- p09-c-27-s04 · Victor: It supports reporting what they say, not what was in the bag. The allegation about the camera is still disputed.
- p09-c-27-s05 · Esme: He allegedly took the camera, then. Does that make the sentence accurate enough?
- p09-c-27-s06 · Victor: Not by itself. Tell readers who made the claim and include his denial; the file gives us both.
- p09-c-27-s07 · Esme: Then I'll write that two witnesses say he left with a black bag, and that he denies taking the camera.
- p09-c-27-s08 · Victor: Use that. It states the accounts without presenting the accusation as a proven event.

目标用法：

- accused · n. · 被告；被指控者；义项 sense-staged-ox-5c1c1c3bf0d1066d-p09-c-27；来源 ox-5c1c1c3bf0d1066d；证据 p09-c-27-s01；词形 accused。
- accusation · n. · 指控；义项 sense-staged-ox-7a7b4b76baff735a-p09-c-27；来源 ox-7a7b4b76baff735a；证据 p09-c-27-s02；词形 accusation。
- allege · v. · 声称；指控但未证实；义项 sense-staged-ox-51088dccbc52deff-p09-c-27；来源 ox-51088dccbc52deff；证据 p09-c-27-s03；词形 allege。
- allegation · n. · 未经证实的指控；义项 sense-staged-ox-c05e4a4e84098954-p09-c-27；来源 ox-c05e4a4e84098954；证据 p09-c-27-s04；词形 allegation。
- allegedly · adv. · 据称；据指控；义项 sense-staged-ox-71bfded8bdf9d909-p09-c-27；来源 ox-71bfded8bdf9d909；证据 p09-c-27-s05；词形 allegedly。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-c；整篇结论：accepted。

课堂报道修订逐步区分摄像机失窃指控、目击黑包的证言及被指控者否认。s07明确提出保留归属和否认的新句，s08选定该句；标题和目标指纠正写法而非证明案件事实，不夸大审理状态。

- ox-7a7b4b76baff735a：accepted；s02的 the accusation 与 not a finding 对照，用名词指尚未被认定事实的指控。（p09-c-27-s02）。

## 找到工作坊管理员并取消重复报名 · p09-c-28

主题：学习与技能；情景：找到工作坊管理员并取消重复报名（workshop-registration-admin-contact）。

沟通目标：确认取消重复报名属于管理事务，保留一个名额并取得取消记录

背景：学员到课程服务台处理自己重复提交的工作坊报名。

人物：Harper、Sol。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-28-s01 · Harper: I seem to have registered twice for the pottery workshop. Can I withdraw the second booking without losing the first?
- p09-c-28-s02 · Sol: Yes. That's an administrative matter rather than a teaching question. I'm the course administrator; I can check both references.
- p09-c-28-s03 · Harper: Here they are. Both are in my name for the same Saturday session.
- p09-c-28-s04 · Sol: I can see the duplicate. Neither booking has a payment attached. Shall I cancel the later one and retain the earlier place?
- p09-c-28-s05 · Harper: Yes, please. I only need one place.
- p09-c-28-s06 · Sol: Done. Here's the cancellation record for the later booking, and the earlier one is still active.
- p09-c-28-s07 · Harper: The two references match. Thank you for sorting it out.

## 商定缺课学生的补课任务调整 · p09-c-29

主题：学习与技能；情景：为缺课学生调整补课任务（teen-class-absence-adjustment）。

沟通目标：核对缺课范围，决定先与学生私下商量缩小补做任务并延后提交

背景：虚构中学中，家长与教师讨论十六岁学生的缺课安排。

人物：Helen、Samir。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-29-s01 · Helen: Maya was absent on Monday and Tuesday. She's worried that she has to redo every classroom activity.
- p09-c-29-s02 · Samir: Her absence means she missed the demonstration, but not the start of a new topic. A small adjustment should be enough.
- p09-c-29-s03 · Helen: As an adolescent, she wants to manage this herself. Could you discuss the plan with her rather than announce it to the class?
- p09-c-29-s04 · Samir: Of course. I'll ask her to watch the demonstration recording and complete just the two related questions.
- p09-c-29-s05 · Helen: Could she hand them in on Friday instead of tomorrow?
- p09-c-29-s06 · Samir: Yes. I'll offer that deadline when I speak to her privately today.
- p09-c-29-s07 · Helen: Thank you. I'll let her know you'll talk with her, not that every detail has already been settled for her.

- absence · n. · 缺席；义项 sense-staged-ox-d65d1a771972231f-p09-c-29；来源 ox-d65d1a771972231f；证据 p09-c-29-s02；词形 absence。
- adjustment · n. · 调整；义项 sense-staged-ox-4eda9da3fa884a10-p09-c-29；来源 ox-4eda9da3fa884a10；证据 p09-c-29-s02；词形 adjustment。
- adolescent · n. · 青少年；义项 sense-staged-ox-2db83cafc2cdf148-p09-c-29；来源 ox-2db83cafc2cdf148；证据 p09-c-29-s03；词形 adolescent。

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-c；整篇结论：accepted。

## 发现台灯电线露出后约定送检 · p09-c-30

主题：居家生活；情景：看到灯线问题后停止调整并约定送检（display-lamp-cable-inspection）。

人物：Yara、Cole。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-30-s01 · Yara: I tilted the shade upwards, and now a wire is visible behind the fitting.
- p09-c-30-s02 · Cole: Is the lamp still plugged in?
- p09-c-30-s03 · Yara: No. I unplugged it before moving it. I haven't touched the wire.
- p09-c-30-s04 · Cole: Let's leave it as it is rather than try to push anything back.
- p09-c-30-s05 · Yara: Agreed. I'll take it to the repair shop and ask them to inspect it.
- p09-c-30-s06 · Cole: I'll use the other lamp tonight. We don't need to test this one ourselves.

## 区分相似手工杯并选定礼物包装 · p09-c-31

主题：消费与服务；情景：区分相似手工杯并选定礼物包装（handmade-mug-gift-identification）。

沟通目标：比较两只外形相似但图案不同的杯子，选定蓝色条纹款并约定用礼盒包装且不在杯身贴胶带

背景：顾客在手工艺店挑选礼物。

人物：Orla、Mateo。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-31-s01 · Orla: These two mugs look alike from here. Are they printed from the same design?
- p09-c-31-s02 · Mateo: No. The shapes match, but each painted pattern is unique. This one has a blue stripe; that one has little blue dots.
- p09-c-31-s03 · Orla: I'll take the stripe. My brother prefers simple patterns.
- p09-c-31-s04 · Mateo: Would you like me to wrap it?
- p09-c-31-s05 · Orla: Yes, but could you avoid sticking tape to the mug? He likes to keep the maker's label.
- p09-c-31-s06 · Mateo: Certainly. I'll put it in this gift box with paper around it and no tape on the mug.
- p09-c-31-s07 · Orla: That will be perfect. The striped one in that box, please.

## 拒绝跳过测试来加快生产 · p09-c-32

主题：工作与协作；情景：拒绝不现实加速并保留检验步骤（production-test-schedule-boundary）。

沟通目标：说明赶工提案不现实，保留既定测试步骤并改为提前准备包装

背景：制造团队讨论虚构内部试产排程，不涉及安全关键系统操作。

人物：Duncan、Mei。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-32-s01 · Duncan: Could we accelerate production by skipping the final test on the trial batch?
- p09-c-32-s02 · Mei: It would be absurd to promise a tested product after removing the test. We need to adhere to the agreed checks.
- p09-c-32-s03 · Duncan: I know. I'm trying to find a way to meet the earlier collection time.
- p09-c-32-s04 · Mei: We can prepare the empty boxes while testing is under way. That saves time without changing what we check.
- p09-c-32-s05 · Duncan: Let's do that. Keep the tests, and I'll organise the packing materials in advance.
- p09-c-32-s06 · Mei: Good. If the batch isn't ready, we'll report that rather than mark an unfinished check as complete.

审阅者：reviewer-phase09-c；整篇结论：accepted。

## 确认社区花园提案的短时会面 · p09-c-33

- p09-c-33-s03 · Alma: Our vision is a place where residents can grow food together. At this stage, we're asking about the process, not approval to build.
- p09-c-33-s04 · Brent: We have a slot on Tuesday at three, albeit a short one. You would have fifteen minutes.
- p09-c-33-s05 · Alma: That's enough for an initial conversation. Could I send a one-page outline first?
- p09-c-33-s06 · Brent: Please do. I'll include it in the briefing and meet you at reception at five to three.
- p09-c-33-s07 · Alma: Thank you. I'll send the outline and come to reception then.

## 预约私下咨询流产相关信息 · p09-c-34

主题：健康与照护；情景：请求私下咨询流产相关信息并确认预约（fictional-private-options-consultation）。

沟通目标：说明希望私下了解流产相关信息且不在前台详谈，确认与临床人员的咨询时间

背景：虚构诊所预约语言练习，不涉及个体化医疗建议或实际服务安排。

人物：Rina、Receptionist。

起草：author-phase09-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p09-c-34-s01 · Rina: I'd like information about abortion, but I don't want to discuss my circumstances at the front desk.
- p09-c-34-s02 · Receptionist: You don't need to explain them here. I can help you arrange a private appointment with a clinician.
- p09-c-34-s03 · Rina: Is the appointment for information, or does booking it mean I've agreed to a procedure?
- p09-c-34-s04 · Receptionist: It's an information appointment. Booking it doesn't commit you to treatment.
- p09-c-34-s05 · Rina: In that case, is Thursday morning available?
- p09-c-34-s06 · Receptionist: Yes, at ten. Would you like that slot?
- p09-c-34-s07 · Rina: Yes, please.
- p09-c-34-s08 · Receptionist: That's booked for Thursday at ten. The clinician will speak with you in a private room.
